```
# Calendar Extension — Visitor Event Sync Visibility Bug

**Date:** 2026-06-23
**Scope:** Why a real visitor event (magic address present, on the host's
**primary** calendar) failed to appear in the Chrome extension's homescreen list,
toolbar badge, and titled nudge — while the in-page DOM nudge still fired (with no
event name). Covers `auxilio-extension` only (client-side sync). The pass-status
server-side issues are a separate audit
(`2026-06-22_calendar_visitor_pass_status_bugs_analysis.md`).
**Type:** Bug analysis + fix. **Implemented 2026-06-23 in extension v0.6.1.**

---

## 1. Reported symptoms

A host had an event with the magic address (`findajx@gmail.com`), 4 attendees
(host + magic address + 2 guests), on their **primary** calendar. In the
extension:

1. **Homescreen** showed "No upcoming visitor events found" even though the event
   existed and was open.
2. After viewing the event and closing the panel, the **in-page nudge** appeared
   but with **no event name** — just "Visitor event needs passes." + Manage.
3. The injected **"Manage Visitors" button** was missing on the first open.

---

## 2. How the surfaces are fed (the relevant data paths)

- **Homescreen list / badge / titled nudge** ← background **sync cache**:
  `useVisitorEvents` → `LIST_VISITOR_EVENTS` → `runSync()` + `listMarked()`
  (`hooks.ts:179`, `background.ts:295`, `lib/calendar-sync.ts`). Sync-derived
  only; **no optimistic fallback**.
- **In-page nudge** ← background sync **∪ an optimistic DOM guess** captured by
  the content script when you view an event whose visible text contains the magic
  address (`calendar.content.ts:239-255`, `mountNudge`/`addOptimistic`).

Key consequence: the nudge can show an event the homescreen does not, because the
nudge has the optimistic path and the homescreen does not.

---

## 3. Root cause — incremental sync never re-evaluates a once-missed event

Diagnostic log captured at repro time:

```
[auxilio] sync done { mode: "incremental", magicAddress: "findajx@gmail.com",
                      itemsReturned: 0, markedTotal: 0, newMarked: 0 }
```

Google Calendar's **incremental** `events.list` (with a `syncToken`) returns only
events that **changed since the token was issued**, and every successful sync
**advances/consumes** the token (`lib/calendar.ts:173-216`). That produces a
permanent blind spot:

1. The event's create/modify delta was consumed by an earlier sync.
2. At that moment `isMarked()` returned **false** — most likely because an earlier
   build used a **different magic address** (`.env` `findajx@gmail.com` vs the
   `.env.example` default `visitors@auxilio.app`), so the address did not match.
3. The token advanced past the delta. From then on incremental sync returns
   `0 items`, so the event is **never re-examined** — even after the address
   config matched again.

`events.get` still resolves the event (so the side panel loads it fine), which is
why only the optimistic DOM nudge fired. The old code only did a full re-scan on a
`410` token expiry (`calendar-sync.ts`), which can be a very long time away — so
the miss was effectively permanent.

**Proven, not guessed:** the optimistic nudge firing means the build's
`MAGIC_ADDRESS` *did* match the event text (so it is not a current mismatch), and
`itemsReturned: 0` / `markedTotal: 0` means the event was simply never in the
synced set — a stale-token miss, not a live `isMarked` failure.

### Secondary defects found in the same flow

- **Nameless nudge:** `snapshotOf` read the title only from an `<input>`, which
  exists on the full editor but **not** the detail popover (the common case), so
  the optimistic nudge had an empty title (`calendar.content.ts` `snapshotOf`).
- **Sync race:** `LIST_VISITOR_EVENTS` called `runSync` with no guard against the
  1-minute alarm's concurrent `doSync` — both read the same baseline and the later
  write clobbered the earlier (lost update on the shared token / marked map).
- **List blanks on a transient error:** a refresh-sync throw made the
  `LIST_VISITOR_EVENTS` query fail, rendering as "no events" rather than serving
  the cached set (`background.ts`).
- **Missing button when panel open:** the floating fallback was suppressed while
  the panel was open, so if the native injection lost the first-open race there
  was **no** button at all (`calendar.content.ts` `render` / `createButtonUI`).

---

## 4. Fix (extension v0.6.1)

**Self-healing full re-scan** (`calendar-sync.ts`, `background.ts`) — the core fix:

- `clearSyncToken()` drops the token + last-full stamp; called from
  `chrome.runtime.onInstalled` so **every install/reload/update** forces a full
  tokenless re-scan. This is what makes a config change (e.g. the magic address)
  actually take effect.
- **Periodic** full re-scan every `FULL_RESYNC_INTERVAL_MS` (12h): if the token is
  older than the interval it is dropped before the sync, so production drift
  self-heals without a reload.
- A full re-scan **rebuilds** the marked set from scratch (stale events drop out)
  but computes "genuinely new" against the **previous** set (`prevMarked`), so it
  does **not** fire a notification storm for events already known.

Bundled secondary fixes:

- Popover title fallback in `snapshotOf` (dialog heading / `aria-label`) → nudge
  shows the event name even before sync reconciles.
- `runSync` coalesced to one in-flight promise → no concurrent-sync lost update.
- `LIST_VISITOR_EVENTS` serves the cached set if the refresh sync throws.
- Floating button fallback kept available when the panel is open.
- Diagnostics: `[auxilio] sync done {…}` per sync, and a `[auxilio] sync MISS …`
  warning when the magic address is in a payload but `isMarked()` is false.

---

## 5. Open item — confirm there is no `events.list` attendee trim

After v0.6.1 forces a full re-scan, two outcomes:

- `markedTotal ≥ 1`, event appears → **fixed** (stale-token miss; this is the
  expected outcome).
- A `[auxilio] sync MISS …` warning appears → Google's `events.list` is returning
  the event with a **trimmed `attendees`** list (a second, distinct cause). Fix
  would be an `events.get` fallback for candidate events to read authoritative
  attendees before deciding `isMarked`.

Once confirmed working, the verbose `sync done` / `sync MISS` logs can be dropped
to a quieter level or gated behind a debug flag.

---

## 6. Related

- Nudge reliability hardening (handled-overlay, auth-lapse banner, recurring
  de-dup): memory `project_extension_nudge_reliability`.
- Secondary-calendar coverage remains **deferred** (primary-only sync,
  `calendar.ts:157-171`); not the cause here (event was on primary).
</content>
</invoke>
```

```
1	/**
2	 * Calendar sync (background only). The durable, official "what changed" engine:
3	 * runs on a chrome.alarm, lists changes via a sync token, and maintains the set
4	 * of upcoming visitor (magic-address) events in storage.local. Drives:
5	 *  - post-save refresh of the open event (changedIds),
6	 *  - the visitor-event notification + toolbar badge (newMarked / count),
7	 *  - the side panel's "open from list" picker (listMarked).
8	 * No DOM. Reflects SAVED state only (pre-save edits are add-on-only — see
9	 * Planning-docs/2026-06-19_calendar_addon_vs_extension_capabilities.md).
10	 */
11	import { MAGIC_ADDRESS } from './config';
12	import { encodeEid, isMarked, listEvents } from './calendar';
13	import type { VisitorEventSummary } from './types';
14	
15	const SYNC_TOKEN_KEY = 'auxilio.syncToken';
16	const MARKED_KEY = 'auxilio.markedEvents';
17	/** Events the user has already issued passes for from this extension — keyed by
18	 *  eventId, value = when handled. A durable overlay over the marked set so a
19	 *  finished event stops badging/notifying/nudging instead of nagging until it
20	 *  ages out. Local-only signal: passes sent via the add-on or another device
21	 *  won't appear here (full cross-channel truth needs an engine status endpoint —
22	 *  deferred). Pruned by HANDLED_TTL_MS. */
23	const HANDLED_KEY = 'auxilio.handledEvents';
24	/** iCalUids the ENGINE reports already have an active pass — i.e. handled through
25	 *  ANY surface (add-on, another device), not just this extension. Overwritten on
26	 *  each status poll (NOT durable like HANDLED_KEY): if passes are later cancelled
27	 *  elsewhere, the next poll drops the uid and the event correctly resurfaces. */
28	const ENGINE_HANDLED_KEY = 'auxilio.engineHandled';
29	/** When the last FULL (tokenless) re-scan ran. Incremental sync only reports
30	 *  changes, so an event missed when its change was first consumed (e.g. a
31	 *  different magic address in an earlier build) is never re-examined until a full
32	 *  re-scan. We force one periodically + on install so the set self-heals. */
33	const LAST_FULL_KEY = 'auxilio.lastFullSync';
34	const PAST_GRACE_MS = 60 * 60_000; // keep events until 1h after they end
35	const HANDLED_TTL_MS = 30 * 24 * 60 * 60_000; // forget "handled" after 30 days
36	const FULL_RESYNC_INTERVAL_MS = 12 * 60 * 60_000; // re-scan the whole window twice a day
37	
38	type MarkedMap = Record<string, VisitorEventSummary>;
39	type HandledMap = Record<string, number>;
40	
41	async function readMarked(): Promise<MarkedMap> {
42	  const r = await chrome.storage.local.get(MARKED_KEY);
43	  return (r[MARKED_KEY] as MarkedMap) ?? {};
44	}
45	
46	async function readHandled(): Promise<HandledMap> {
47	  const r = await chrome.storage.local.get(HANDLED_KEY);
48	  return (r[HANDLED_KEY] as HandledMap) ?? {};
49	}
50	
51	/** Drop the sync token so the next sync does a full tokenless re-scan of the
52	 *  forward window. Called on install/update so a config change (e.g. the magic
53	 *  address) takes effect immediately instead of waiting for the periodic resync. */
54	export async function clearSyncToken(): Promise<void> {
55	  await chrome.storage.local.remove([SYNC_TOKEN_KEY, LAST_FULL_KEY]);
56	}
57	
58	/** Record that passes were issued for this event — drops it from every nudge
59	 *  surface (badge, banner, notification, picker) until it ages out. */
60	export async function markHandled(eventId: string): Promise<void> {
61	  if (!eventId) return;
62	  const handled = await readHandled();
63	  handled[eventId] = Date.now();
64	  await chrome.storage.local.set({ [HANDLED_KEY]: handled });
65	}
66	
67	export async function readEngineHandled(): Promise<Set<string>> {
68	  const r = await chrome.storage.local.get(ENGINE_HANDLED_KEY);
69	  return new Set((r[ENGINE_HANDLED_KEY] as string[]) ?? []);
70	}
71	
72	/** iCalUids of marked events that aren't LOCALLY handled — the candidate set to
73	 *  ask the engine about. Deliberately ignores engineHandled: we must keep asking
74	 *  about already-engine-handled events so a pass cancelled elsewhere is re-detected
75	 *  and the event resurfaces. */
76	export async function listPendingICalUids(): Promise<string[]> {
77	  const [marked, handled] = await Promise.all([readMarked(), readHandled()]);
78	  const uids = new Set<string>();
79	  for (const m of Object.values(marked)) {
80	    if (handled[m.eventId]) continue;
81	    if (m.iCalUid) uids.add(m.iCalUid);
82	  }
83	  return [...uids];
84	}
85	
86	/** Replace the engine-handled iCalUid set with the latest status answer. Overwrite
87	 *  (not merge) so events whose passes were cancelled elsewhere drop out and
88	 *  resurface. Lower-cased for case-insensitive matching against marked iCalUids. */
89	export async function setEngineHandled(iCalUids: string[]): Promise<void> {
90	  await chrome.storage.local.set({
91	    [ENGINE_HANDLED_KEY]: [...new Set(iCalUids.map((u) => u.toLowerCase()))],
92	  });
93	}
94	
95	export interface SyncResult {
96	  changedIds: Set<string>;
97	  newMarked: VisitorEventSummary[];
98	  /** Events seen this cycle that are no longer pending visitor events (deleted/
99	   *  cancelled). Lets the in-page nudge purge stale optimistic guesses. */
100	  cancelledIds: string[];
101	  markedCount: number;
102	}
103	
104	/** Coalesce concurrent syncs (the 1-minute alarm + on-demand LIST/SYNC_NOW) so
105	 *  they can't race on the shared sync token / marked map and lose an update — both
106	 *  read the same baseline, both write at the end, and the later write clobbers the
107	 *  earlier one (an event one run marked can vanish, and won't be re-reported once
108	 *  the token advances). One in-flight sync at a time removes that hazard. */
109	let inFlight: Promise<SyncResult> | null = null;
110	export function runSync(accessToken: string): Promise<SyncResult> {
111	  if (inFlight) return inFlight;
112	  inFlight = doRunSync(accessToken).finally(() => {
113	    inFlight = null;
114	  });
115	  return inFlight;
116	}
117	
118	/** Pull changes since the last sync; update the marked set; report deltas. */
119	async function doRunSync(accessToken: string): Promise<SyncResult> {
120	  const store = await chrome.storage.local.get([
121	    SYNC_TOKEN_KEY,
122	    MARKED_KEY,
123	    HANDLED_KEY,
124	    ENGINE_HANDLED_KEY,
125	    LAST_FULL_KEY,
126	  ]);
127	  const engineHandled = new Set((store[ENGINE_HANDLED_KEY] as string[]) ?? []);
128	  // The set we knew BEFORE this sync — the baseline for "what's genuinely new"
129	  // (so a full re-scan that re-discovers the whole window doesn't re-notify).
130	  const prevMarked = (store[MARKED_KEY] as MarkedMap) ?? {};
131	  const handled = (store[HANDLED_KEY] as HandledMap) ?? {};
132	  const lastFull = (store[LAST_FULL_KEY] as number) ?? 0;
133	
134	  let syncToken = store[SYNC_TOKEN_KEY] as string | undefined;
135	  // Force a periodic full re-scan: incremental sync never re-reports an event
136	  // whose change was already consumed, so a once-missed event (wrong magic
137	  // address in a past build, a transient attendee trim) would stay invisible
138	  // forever. Dropping the token re-evaluates the whole forward window.
139	  if (syncToken && Date.now() - lastFull > FULL_RESYNC_INTERVAL_MS) {
140	    syncToken = undefined;
141	  }
142	
143	  let resp = await listEvents(accessToken, { syncToken });
144	  if (resp.expired) {
145	    // Token stale → full resync from scratch.
146	    syncToken = undefined;
147	    resp = await listEvents(accessToken, {});
148	  }
149	  // A tokenless call re-scanned the full window; rebuild the set from scratch so
150	  // stale/no-longer-marked events drop out. Incremental carries the set forward.
151	  const fullSync = !syncToken;
152	  const marked: MarkedMap = fullSync ? {} : { ...prevMarked };
153	
154	  const changedIds = new Set<string>();
155	  const newMarked: VisitorEventSummary[] = [];
156	  const cancelledIds: string[] = [];
157	
158	  // Series we already tracked (any previously-marked instance) — so a newly
159	  // discovered later occurrence of a known recurring event, OR a full re-scan
160	  // that re-finds the whole window, doesn't fire a fresh notification.
161	  const knownSeries = new Set(
162	    Object.values(prevMarked).map((m) => m.seriesId ?? m.eventId),
163	  );
164	  // Series notified within THIS cycle — collapses an initial sync that discovers
165	  // dozens of weekly instances at once down to a single notification.
166	  const notifiedSeries = new Set<string>();
167	
168	  for (const ev of resp.items) {
169	    if (!ev.id) continue;
170	    changedIds.add(ev.id);
171	    const cancelled = ev.status === 'cancelled';
172	    const marked_ = isMarked(ev);
173	    // DIAGNOSTIC: the magic address is somewhere in this event's payload, yet
174	    // isMarked() didn't flag it — i.e. it's not a structured attendee email and
175	    // not in `location` (e.g. it's only in the description, or events.list
176	    // returned a trimmed attendee list). This is the signature of a primary-
177	    // calendar miss; the warning shows exactly what we got back.
178	    if (!marked_ && MAGIC_ADDRESS && JSON.stringify(ev).toLowerCase().includes(MAGIC_ADDRESS)) {
179	      console.warn(
180	        '[auxilio] sync MISS: magic address in payload but isMarked() is false',
181	        { id: ev.id, summary: ev.summary, location: ev.location, attendees: ev.attendees, status: ev.status },
182	      );
183	    }
184	    if (marked_ && !cancelled) {
185	      const seriesId = ev.recurringEventId || ev.id;
186	      const summary: VisitorEventSummary = {
187	        eid: encodeEid(ev.id, 'primary'),
188	        eventId: ev.id,
189	        iCalUid: ev.iCalUID ?? '',
190	        title: ev.summary || '(no title)',
191	        start: ev.start?.dateTime ?? ev.start?.date,
192	        seriesId,
193	      };
194	      // Notify only for a genuinely new, not-yet-handled occurrence of a series we
195	      // weren't already tracking — one alert per recurring series, not per
196	      // instance, and never for an event a full re-scan merely re-discovered.
197	      const newInstance = !prevMarked[ev.id] && !handled[ev.id];
198	      if (newInstance && !knownSeries.has(seriesId) && !notifiedSeries.has(seriesId)) {
199	        newMarked.push(summary);
200	        notifiedSeries.add(seriesId);
201	      }
202	      marked[ev.id] = summary;
203	    } else {
204	      // Cancelled (deleted) or no longer a visitor event → not pending. Report
205	      // it so any optimistic in-page nudge for it gets purged, and forget any
206	      // "handled" record so the id can't linger.
207	      if (cancelled || prevMarked[ev.id]) cancelledIds.push(ev.id);
208	      delete marked[ev.id];
209	      delete handled[ev.id];
210	    }
211	  }
212	
213	  // Prune events that are well past.
214	  const cutoff = Date.now() - PAST_GRACE_MS;
215	  for (const id of Object.keys(marked)) {
216	    const s = marked[id].start;
217	    if (s && Date.parse(s) < cutoff) delete marked[id];
218	  }
219	
220	  // Prune stale "handled" records so the overlay can't grow without bound.
221	  const handledCutoff = Date.now() - HANDLED_TTL_MS;
222	  for (const id of Object.keys(handled)) {
223	    if (handled[id] < handledCutoff) delete handled[id];
224	  }
225	
226	  // DIAGNOSTIC: one line per sync so a primary-calendar miss is obvious — did the
227	  // list even return the event (itemsReturned), and did anything end up marked?
228	  console.log('[auxilio] sync done', {
229	    mode: fullSync ? 'full' : 'incremental',
230	    magicAddress: MAGIC_ADDRESS,
231	    itemsReturned: resp.items.length,
232	    markedTotal: Object.keys(marked).length,
233	    newMarked: newMarked.length,
234	  });
235	
236	  await chrome.storage.local.set({
237	    [SYNC_TOKEN_KEY]: resp.nextSyncToken ?? syncToken,
238	    [MARKED_KEY]: marked,
239	    [HANDLED_KEY]: handled,
240	    // Stamp the full-resync clock only when we actually did one, so the periodic
241	    // re-scan fires on schedule regardless of how often incremental sync runs.
242	    ...(fullSync ? { [LAST_FULL_KEY]: Date.now() } : {}),
243	  });
244	
245	  // markedCount drives the toolbar badge — count distinct pending series (a
246	  // recurring event counts once, not once per occurrence), excluding events the
247	  // engine already handled elsewhere. Uses the engineHandled set from the start
248	  // of this sync; the post-sync status poll reconciles the badge if it changed.
249	  const pendingSeries = new Set<string>();
250	  for (const id of Object.keys(marked)) {
251	    if (handled[id]) continue;
252	    if (engineHandled.has((marked[id].iCalUid ?? '').toLowerCase())) continue;
253	    pendingSeries.add(marked[id].seriesId ?? id);
254	  }
255	  return { changedIds, newMarked, cancelledIds, markedCount: pendingSeries.size };
256	}
257	
258	/** Upcoming visitor events still needing passes, soonest first — for the panel's
259	 *  picker and the in-page nudge. Handled events are filtered out, and a recurring
260	 *  series collapses to its soonest pending occurrence (one row, not one per week). */
261	export async function listMarked(): Promise<VisitorEventSummary[]> {
262	  const [marked, handled, engineHandled] = await Promise.all([
263	    readMarked(),
264	    readHandled(),
265	    readEngineHandled(),
266	  ]);
267	  const bySeries = new Map<string, VisitorEventSummary>();
268	  for (const m of Object.values(marked)) {
269	    if (handled[m.eventId]) continue;
270	    if (engineHandled.has((m.iCalUid ?? '').toLowerCase())) continue;
271	    const key = m.seriesId ?? m.eventId;
272	    const existing = bySeries.get(key);
273	    if (!existing || (m.start ?? '~').localeCompare(existing.start ?? '~') < 0) {
274	      bySeries.set(key, m);
275	    }
276	  }
277	  return [...bySeries.values()].sort((a, b) =>
278	    (a.start ?? '').localeCompare(b.start ?? ''),
279	  );
280	}
281	
282	/** Is this event id currently a known visitor event? */
283	export async function isEventMarked(eventId: string): Promise<boolean> {
284	  const marked = await readMarked();
285	  return !!marked[eventId];
286	}
287
```

```
18	export function useActiveEid(): string | null {

22	      .get(ACTIVE_EID_KEY)
23	      .then((r) => setEid((r[ACTIVE_EID_KEY] as string) ?? null));

30	      }

108	/**
109	 * Background-enrich the draft: for guests we only have an email-derived fallback
110	 * name for (`nameIsFallback`), resolve the real name + photo from the host's Google
111	 * contacts and merge them into the cached draft. Non-destructive — it only rewrites
112	 * cache fields; RosterRow adopts an updated name/photo solely when the host hasn't
113	 * edited that field, so a late resolve never clobbers a live correction. One-shot
114	 * per distinct set of pending guests (cached in the background after the first run).
115	 */
116	export function useResolveGuestNames(draft: DraftResponse | undefined) {
117	  const qc = useQueryClient();
118	  const iCalUid = draft?.iCalUid;
119	  const pending = (draft?.roster ?? [])
120	    .filter((g) => g.nameIsFallback)
121	    .map((g) => g.email.toLowerCase())
122	    .sort();
123	  // Stable key so the effect fires only when the pending set actually changes.
124	  const key = pending.join('|');
125	
126	  useEffect(() => {
127	    if (!iCalUid || pending.length === 0) return;
128	    let cancelled = false;
129	    rpc({ type: 'RESOLVE_GUESTS', emails: pending })
130	      .then((resolved) => {
131	        if (cancelled || !resolved || Object.keys(resolved).length === 0) return;
132	        qc.setQueryData<DraftResponse>(['draft', iCalUid], (prev) => {
133	          if (!prev) return prev;
134	          return {
135	            ...prev,
136	            roster: prev.roster.map((g) => {
137	              const hit = resolved[g.email.toLowerCase()];
138	              if (!hit) return g;
139	              return {
140	                ...g,
141	                name: hit.name ?? g.name,
142	                photoUrl: hit.photoUrl ?? g.photoUrl,
143	                // A real name resolved → it's no longer a fallback.
144	                nameIsFallback: hit.name ? false : g.nameIsFallback,
145	              };
146	            }),
147	          };
148	        });
149	      })
150	      .catch(() => {
151	        /* name resolution is best-effort — failures keep the fallback name */
152	      });
153	    return () => {
154	      cancelled = true;
155	    };
156	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
157	  }, [iCalUid, key]);
158	}
159	
160	/** Merge a returned VisitDraft into the cached DraftResponse (keep extras). */
161	function mergeDraft(
162	  prev: DraftResponse | undefined,
163	  next: VisitDraft,
164	): DraftResponse | undefined {
165	  if (!prev) return prev;
166	  return { ...prev, ...next };
167	}
168	
169	/** Optimistic patch (toggle / name / phone / template / location). */
170	export function usePatchDraft(iCalUid: string | undefined) {
171	  const qc = useQueryClient();
172	  const key = ['draft', iCalUid];
173	  return useMutation({
174	    mutationFn: (patch: DraftPatch) =>
175	      rpc({ type: 'DRAFT_PATCH', iCalUid: iCalUid!, patch }),
176	    onMutate: async (patch) => {
177	      await qc.cancelQueries({ queryKey: key });
178	      const prev = qc.getQueryData<DraftResponse>(key);
179	      if (prev) qc.setQueryData<DraftResponse>(key, applyPatch(prev, patch));
180	      return { prev };
181	    },
182	    onError: (_e, _patch, ctx) => {
183	      if (ctx?.prev) qc.setQueryData(key, ctx.prev);
184	    },
185	    onSuccess: (updated) => {
186	      qc.setQueryData<DraftResponse>(key, (prev) => mergeDraft(prev, updated));
187	    },
188	  });
189	}
190	
191	/** Apply a DraftPatch to a cached DraftResponse for the optimistic view. */
192	function applyPatch(draft: DraftResponse, patch: DraftPatch): DraftResponse {
193	  const roster = draft.roster.map((g) => {
194	    const edit = patch.guests?.find(
195	      (e) => e.email.toLowerCase() === g.email.toLowerCase(),
196	    );
197	    return edit ? { ...g, ...stripEmail(edit) } : g;
198	  });
199	  return {
200	    ...draft,
201	    roster,
202	    location: patch.location ?? draft.location,
203	  };
204	}
205	
206	function stripEmail<T extends { email: string }>(e: T): Omit<T, 'email'> {
207	  const { email: _email, ...rest } = e;
208	  return rest;
209	}
210

215	    onSuccess: (res) => {
```

```
1	/**
2	 * Calendar read (background only). Two jobs:
3	 *  1) resolve the canonical event for a given eid (events.get) — authoritative
4	 *     iCalUID + attendees for the side panel.
5	 *  2) list changes via a sync token (events.list) — the durable "what changed"
6	 *     API that drives post-save refresh + the visitor-event nudge/badge.
7	 * Calendar.events.readonly only.
8	 */
9	import { MAGIC_ADDRESS } from './config';
10	import type { ActiveEvent } from './types';
11	
12	/** `eid` is URL-safe base64 of "<eventId> <calendarId>" (calendarId optional). */
13	export function decodeEid(eid: string): { eventId: string; calendarId: string } | null {
14	  try {
15	    let b64 = eid.replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/');
16	    b64 += '='.repeat((4 - (b64.length % 4)) % 4);
17	    const decoded = atob(b64);
18	    const [eventId, calendarId] = decoded.split(' ');
19	    if (!eventId) return null;
20	    return { eventId, calendarId: calendarId || 'primary' };
21	  } catch {
22	    return null;
23	  }
24	}
25	
26	/** Inverse of decodeEid — build an eid the side panel can resolve. */
27	export function encodeEid(eventId: string, calendarId = 'primary'): string {
28	  const b64 = btoa(`${eventId} ${calendarId}`);
29	  return b64.replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=+$/, '');
30	}
31	
32	export interface GCalEvent {
33	  id?: string;
34	  iCalUID?: string;
35	  status?: string;
36	  summary?: string;
37	  location?: string;
38	  description?: string;
39	  /** Present on instances of a recurring series (with singleEvents=true): the id
40	   *  of the parent series. Lets us treat a series as one nudge unit. */
41	  recurringEventId?: string;
42	  start?: { dateTime?: string; date?: string };
43	  end?: { dateTime?: string; date?: string };
44	  organizer?: { email?: string };
45	  attendees?: {
46	    email?: string;
47	    displayName?: string;
48	    resource?: boolean;
49	    self?: boolean;
50	    organizer?: boolean;
51	  }[];
52	}
53	
54	/** Is this a visitor event? (magic address among attendees or in the location.) */
55	export function isMarked(ev: GCalEvent): boolean {
56	  if (!MAGIC_ADDRESS) return false;
57	  const inAttendees = (ev.attendees ?? []).some(
58	    (a) => a.email?.toLowerCase() === MAGIC_ADDRESS,
59	  );
60	  const inLocation = (ev.location ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(MAGIC_ADDRESS);
61	  return inAttendees || inLocation;
62	}
63	
64	function startOf(ev: GCalEvent): string | undefined {
65	  return ev.start?.dateTime ?? ev.start?.date;
66	}
67	
68	async function getEvent(
69	  calendarId: string,
70	  eventId: string,
71	  accessToken: string,
72	): Promise<Response> {
73	  const url = new URL(
74	    `https://www.googleapis.com/calendar/v3/calendars/${encodeURIComponent(
75	      calendarId,
76	    )}/events/${encodeURIComponent(eventId)}`,
77	  );
78	  url.searchParams.set(
79	    'fields',
80	    'iCalUID,summary,location,description,start,end,organizer,attendees(email,displayName,resource,self,organizer)',
81	  );
82	  return fetch(url, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}` } });
83	}
84	
85	export async function fetchActiveEvent(
86	  eid: string,
87	  accessToken: string,
88	): Promise<ActiveEvent> {
89	  const dec = decodeEid(eid);
90	  if (!dec) throw new Error('Could not read the event id from this page.');
91	
92	  // Recurring chips carry an instance id ("<base>_<ts>"); events.get often 404s
93	  // on the instance, so also try the base id. And try `primary` in case the
94	  // decoded calendar id isn't directly fetchable.
95	  const baseId = dec.eventId.includes('_') ? dec.eventId.split('_')[0] : null;
96	  const candidates: { cal: string; id: string }[] = [
97	    { cal: dec.calendarId, id: dec.eventId },
98	    ...(dec.calendarId !== 'primary' ? [{ cal: 'primary', id: dec.eventId }] : []),
99	    ...(baseId ? [{ cal: dec.calendarId, id: baseId }] : []),
100	    ...(baseId && dec.calendarId !== 'primary' ? [{ cal: 'primary', id: baseId }] : []),
101	  ];
102	  console.log('[auxilio] resolve event', { eid, ...dec, candidates });
103	
104	  let res: Response | null = null;
105	  let lastStatus = 0;
106	  let lastBody = '';
107	  for (const c of candidates) {
108	    const r = await getEvent(c.cal, c.id, accessToken);
109	    if (r.ok) {
110	      res = r;
111	      break;
112	    }
113	    lastStatus = r.status;
114	    lastBody = await r.text().catch(() => '');
115	    console.warn('[auxilio] events.get miss', c, r.status, lastBody.slice(0, 200));
116	  }
117	
118	  if (!res) {
119	    console.warn('[auxilio] resolve 404', { ...dec, lastBody: lastBody.slice(0, 200) });
120	    if (lastStatus === 404) {
121	      // Most common cause: a brand-new event that hasn't been saved yet — it
122	      // doesn't exist on Google's servers, so there's nothing to fetch.
123	      throw new Error('NOT_SAVED');
124	    }
125	    throw new Error(`Calendar API error (${lastStatus}). ${lastBody.slice(0, 120)}`);
126	  }
127	  const ev = (await res.json()) as GCalEvent;
128	  console.log('[auxilio] resolved event', {
129	    iCalUID: ev.iCalUID,
130	    summary: ev.summary,
131	    attendees: ev.attendees?.length ?? 0,
132	  });
133	
134	  const attendees = (ev.attendees ?? [])
135	    .filter((a) => a.email && !a.resource && a.email.toLowerCase() !== MAGIC_ADDRESS)
136	    .map((a) => ({ email: a.email as string, name: a.displayName }));
137	
138	  const rooms = (ev.attendees ?? [])
139	    .filter((a) => a.resource)
140	    .map((a) => a.displayName || a.email || '')
141	    .filter(Boolean);
142	
143	  return {
144	    iCalUid: ev.iCalUID ?? '',
145	    providerEventId: dec.eventId,
146	    title: ev.summary,
147	    start: startOf(ev),
148	    end: ev.end?.dateTime ?? ev.end?.date,
149	    location: ev.location,
150	    description: ev.description,
151	    rooms,
152	    organizerEmail: ev.organizer?.email,
153	    attendees,
154	  };
155	}
156	
157	/**
158	 * List changes on the primary calendar. With a sync token → only what changed
159	 * since last sync; without → an initial window of upcoming events (and a fresh
160	 * token). Paginates to capture nextSyncToken. Returns expired:true on a 410 so
161	 * the caller can full-resync.
162	 *
163	 * KNOWN LIMITATION (deferred by decision): primary calendar only. A visitor event
164	 * a host creates on a *secondary* calendar they own gets no sync-driven nudge
165	 * (badge/notification/banner) — the optimistic DOM path still fires if they open
166	 * it. Covering owned secondaries needs the calendar.calendarlist.readonly scope
167	 * (to discover them via calendarList.list) plus a per-calendar sync token. We
168	 * deferred this: the gap is unobserved and the extra scope/poll cost isn't worth
169	 * a maybe. Revisit only on a real report of a missed secondary-calendar event,
170	 * and when doing so, hard-filter to accessRole=owner (never poll subscribed/
171	 * holiday calendars).
172	 */
173	export async function listEvents(
174	  accessToken: string,
175	  opts: { syncToken?: string } = {},
176	): Promise<{ items: GCalEvent[]; nextSyncToken?: string; expired?: boolean }> {
177	  const items: GCalEvent[] = [];
178	  let pageToken: string | undefined;
179	  let nextSyncToken: string | undefined;
180	
181	  do {
182	    const url = new URL(
183	      'https://www.googleapis.com/calendar/v3/calendars/primary/events',
184	    );
185	    url.searchParams.set('singleEvents', 'true');
186	    url.searchParams.set('showDeleted', 'true');
187	    url.searchParams.set('maxResults', '250');
188	    url.searchParams.set(
189	      'fields',
190	      'items(id,iCalUID,status,summary,location,recurringEventId,start,end,attendees(email,resource)),nextPageToken,nextSyncToken',
191	    );
192	    if (opts.syncToken) {
193	      url.searchParams.set('syncToken', opts.syncToken);
194	    } else {
195	      // Initial sync: a forward window keeps the token small and relevant.
196	      url.searchParams.set('timeMin', new Date().toISOString());
197	    }
198	    if (pageToken) url.searchParams.set('pageToken', pageToken);
199	
200	    const res = await fetch(url, {
201	      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}` },
202	    });
203	    if (res.status === 410) return { items: [], expired: true }; // sync token stale
204	    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Calendar list error (${res.status}).`);
205	    const data = (await res.json()) as {
206	      items?: GCalEvent[];
207	      nextPageToken?: string;
208	      nextSyncToken?: string;
209	    };
210	    if (data.items) items.push(...data.items);
211	    pageToken = data.nextPageToken;
212	    nextSyncToken = data.nextSyncToken ?? nextSyncToken;
213	  } while (pageToken);
214	
215	  return { items, nextSyncToken };
216	}
217
```

```
1	/**
2	 * Auth (background only). Mints a Google id_token (engine auth) + access_token
3	 * (Calendar read) via chrome.identity.launchWebAuthFlow against a Web OAuth
4	 * client, caches them in storage.session, and silently re-mints on expiry.
5	 *
6	 * The id_token's audience must be on the engine's AUXILIO_WORKSPACE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID
7	 * allow-list. Calendar-only scopes (no Gmail/Drive — CASA line).
8	 */
9	import { OAUTH_CLIENT_ID } from './config';
10	import { engine } from './engine';
11	import type { AuthStatus } from './types';
12	
13	const SCOPES = [
14	  'openid',
15	  'email',
16	  'profile',
17	  'https://www.googleapis.com/auth/calendar.events.readonly',
18	  // Resolve guest names + photos the Calendar event API omits for external guests
19	  // (People API, client-side — mirrors the add-on). Both are *sensitive* scopes,
20	  // NOT restricted → no CASA, and still no Gmail/Drive. Adding these requires each
21	  // user to re-consent once (a silent renew won't widen scope).
22	  'https://www.googleapis.com/auth/contacts.readonly',
23	  'https://www.googleapis.com/auth/contacts.other.readonly',
24	].join(' ');
25	
26	const KEY = 'auxilio.tokens';
27	
28	/** Durable (storage.local) marker that the user has connected and intends to stay
29	 *  connected. Token bundles live in storage.session and are WIPED on every browser
30	 *  restart, so an absent bundle can't tell "never signed in" from "session cleared,
31	 *  silent renew due." This flag survives restarts: while it's set, a failed silent
32	 *  renew means auth has lapsed (surface a reconnect), not that the user is signed
33	 *  out. Cleared only on explicit sign-out. */
34	const CONNECTED_KEY = 'auxilio.connected';
35	
36	interface TokenBundle {
37	  idToken: string;
38	  accessToken: string;
39	  email?: string;
40	  expiresAt: number;
41	}
42	
43	function parseFragment(redirectUrl: string) {
44	  const params = new URLSearchParams(new URL(redirectUrl).hash.slice(1));
45	  return {
46	    accessToken: params.get('access_token') ?? '',
47	    idToken: params.get('id_token') ?? '',
48	    code: params.get('code') ?? '',
49	    expiresIn: Number(params.get('expires_in') ?? '3600'),
50	    error: params.get('error') ?? undefined,
51	  };
52	}
53	
54	function emailFromIdToken(idToken: string): string | undefined {
55	  try {
56	    const part = idToken.split('.')[1].replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/');
57	    return JSON.parse(atob(part)).email as string | undefined;
58	  } catch {
59	    return undefined;
60	  }
61	}
62	
63	async function readCache(): Promise<TokenBundle | null> {
64	  const r = await chrome.storage.session.get(KEY);
65	  return (r[KEY] as TokenBundle) ?? null;
66	}
67	
68	async function mint(interactive: boolean): Promise<{ bundle: TokenBundle; code?: string }> {
69	  if (!OAUTH_CLIENT_ID) {
70	    throw new Error(
71	      'OAuth client id missing. Set WXT_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID in .env and rebuild.',
72	    );
73	  }
74	  const url = new URL('https://accounts.google.com/o/oauth2/v2/auth');
75	  url.searchParams.set('client_id', OAUTH_CLIENT_ID);
76	  // Interactive: hybrid flow (+ offline) so we ALSO get a code to hand the engine
77	  // for a calendar refresh token. Silent renew stays implicit (client tokens only).
78	  url.searchParams.set('response_type', interactive ? 'code token id_token' : 'token id_token');
79	  url.searchParams.set('redirect_uri', chrome.identity.getRedirectURL());
80	  url.searchParams.set('scope', SCOPES);
81	  url.searchParams.set('nonce', crypto.randomUUID());
82	  url.searchParams.set('prompt', interactive ? 'consent select_account' : 'none');
83	  if (interactive) url.searchParams.set('access_type', 'offline');
84	
85	  const redirect = await chrome.identity.launchWebAuthFlow({
86	    url: url.toString(),
87	    interactive,
88	  });
89	  if (!redirect) throw new Error('Sign-in was cancelled');
90	
91	  const { accessToken, idToken, code, expiresIn, error } = parseFragment(redirect);
92	  if (error) throw new Error(`Google sign-in error: ${error}`);
93	  if (!idToken || !accessToken) throw new Error('Sign-in did not return tokens');
94	
95	  const bundle: TokenBundle = {
96	    idToken,
97	    accessToken,
98	    email: emailFromIdToken(idToken),
99	    expiresAt: Date.now() + (expiresIn - 60) * 1000, // refresh 60s early
100	  };
101	  await chrome.storage.session.set({ [KEY]: bundle });
102	  return { bundle, code: code || undefined };
103	}
104	
105	/** Valid tokens, silently re-minting if expired. Throws if interactive sign-in
106	 *  is required (caller surfaces a sign-in gate). */
107	export async function getValidTokens(): Promise<TokenBundle> {
108	  const cached = await readCache();
109	  if (cached && cached.expiresAt > Date.now()) return cached;
110	  return (await mint(false)).bundle; // prompt=none silent renew
111	}
112	
113	export async function signIn(): Promise<AuthStatus> {
114	  const { bundle, code } = await mint(true);
115	  // Mark "connected" durably so a later silent-renew failure reads as a lapse to
116	  // recover from, not a sign-out (see CONNECTED_KEY).
117	  await chrome.storage.local.set({ [CONNECTED_KEY]: true });
118	  // Best-effort: hand the auth code to the engine so it can mint a calendar
119	  // refresh token and keep passes in sync when the panel is closed. Never let a
120	  // failure here break sign-in — the engine's connectUrl CTA remains the fallback.
121	  if (code) {
122	    await engine
123	      .connectCalendar(bundle.idToken, code, chrome.identity.getRedirectURL())
124	      .catch((err) => console.warn('[auxilio] calendar connect failed', err));
125	  }
126	  return { signedIn: true, email: bundle.email };
127	}
128	
129	export async function signOut(): Promise<void> {
130	  const cached = await readCache();
131	  if (cached?.accessToken) {
132	    await fetch(
133	      `https://oauth2.googleapis.com/revoke?token=${cached.accessToken}`,
134	      { method: 'POST' },
135	    ).catch(() => {});
136	  }
137	  await chrome.storage.session.remove(KEY);
138	  await chrome.storage.local.remove(CONNECTED_KEY);
139	}
140	
141	export async function authStatus(): Promise<AuthStatus> {
142	  const cached = await readCache();
143	  if (cached && cached.expiresAt > Date.now()) {
144	    return { signedIn: true, email: cached.email };
145	  }
146	  return { signedIn: false };
147	}
148	
149	/** Did the user connect at least once and not explicitly sign out? Survives the
150	 *  restart that wipes the storage.session token cache — so the background can tell
151	 *  a recoverable auth lapse from a genuine signed-out state. */
152	export async function wasConnected(): Promise<boolean> {
153	  const r = await chrome.storage.local.get(CONNECTED_KEY);
154	  return !!r[CONNECTED_KEY];
155	}
156
```

```
230	function fail(err: unknown): RpcResponse<never> {
231	  const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
232	  const status = err instanceof EngineError ? err.status : undefined;
233	  return { ok: false, error: message, status, needsAuth: status === 401 };
234	}
235	
236	async function withTokens<T>(
237	  fn: (t: { idToken: string; accessToken: string; email?: string }) => Promise<T>,
238	): Promise<RpcResponse<T>> {
239	  let tokens;
240	  try {
241	    tokens = await getValidTokens();
242	  } catch {
243	    return { ok: false, error: 'Sign in to continue.', needsAuth: true };
244	  }
245	  try {
246	    return ok(await fn(tokens));
247	  } catch (err) {
248	    return fail(err);
249	  }
250	}
251	
252	const withIdToken = <T,>(fn: (idToken: string) => Promise<T>) =>
253	  withTokens((t) => fn(t.idToken));

255	async function handle(
256	  msg: RpcRequest,
257	  sender: chrome.runtime.MessageSender,
258	): Promise<RpcResponse<unknown>> {
259	  switch (msg.type) {
260	    case 'AUTH_STATUS':
261	      return ok(await authStatus());
262	    case 'AUTH_SIGN_IN': {
263	      const status = await signIn();
264	      void doSync(); // warm the marked set + badge right after sign-in
265	      return ok(status);
266	    }
267	    case 'AUTH_SIGN_OUT':
268	      await signOut();
269	      await chrome.action.setBadgeText({ text: '' });
270	      return ok({ signedIn: false });
271	
272	    case 'OPEN_PANEL': {
273	      // Open the side panel within the in-page click's user activation (e.g. the
274	      // "Reconnect" banner) so the user lands on the sign-in gate. No active event.
275	      const tabId = sender.tab?.id;
276	      let opened = false;
277	      if (!panelConnected && tabId != null) {
278	        try {
279	          await chrome.sidePanel.open({ tabId });
280	          opened = true;
281	        } catch (err) {
282	          console.warn('[auxilio] sidePanel.open failed (gesture not carried?)', err);
283	        }
284	      }
285	      return ok({ opened });
286	    }
287	
288	    case 'OPEN_FOR_EVENT': {
289	      // The background is the single authority on open-vs-follow: panelConnected
290	      // (the panel's own port) is the truth, so the content script no longer
291	      // guesses with local flags that drift. Open the panel only when it isn't
292	      // already connected — otherwise this is just a "follow" and we fall through
293	      // to update the active event below. Call sidePanel.open FIRST, before any
294	      // await, so the content-script click's user activation is still valid
295	      // (sidePanel.open requires a live gesture).
296	      const tabId = sender.tab?.id;
297	      let opened = false;
298	      if (!panelConnected && tabId != null) {
299	        try {
300	          await chrome.sidePanel.open({ tabId });
301	          opened = true;
302	        } catch (err) {
303	          console.warn('[auxilio] sidePanel.open failed (gesture not carried?)', err);
304	        }
305	      }
306	      // Store after — the panel reacts to this via storage.onChanged either way.
307	      // The DOM snapshot lets the panel paint instantly + cover the unsaved case.
308	      await chrome.storage.session.set({
309	        [ACTIVE_EID_KEY]: msg.eid,
310	        [ACTIVE_SNAPSHOT_KEY]: msg.snapshot ?? null,
311	      });
312	      return ok({ opened });
313	    }
314	
315	    case 'FOLLOW_EVENT': {
316	      // Auto-follow: point the (already open) panel at the event the user is
317	      // viewing — no sidePanel.open. The panel applies its own busy-guard.
318	      await chrome.storage.session.set({
319	        [ACTIVE_EID_KEY]: msg.eid,
320	        [ACTIVE_SNAPSHOT_KEY]: msg.snapshot ?? null,
321	      });
322	      return ok({ followed: true });
323	    }
324	
325	    case 'RESOLVE_EVENT':
326	      return withTokens((t) => fetchActiveEvent(msg.eid, t.accessToken));
327	
328	    case 'DRAFT_LOAD':
329	      return withIdToken((t) => engine.loadDraft(t, msg.event));
330	    case 'RESOLVE_GUESTS':
331	      // Best-effort People API name/photo lookup with the host's access_token.
332	      return withTokens((t) => resolveGuests(t.accessToken, msg.emails));
333	    case 'DRAFT_PATCH':
334	      return withIdToken((t) => engine.patchDraft(t, msg.iCalUid, msg.patch));
335	    case 'PREVIEW':
336	      return withIdToken((t) => engine.preview(t, msg.iCalUid, msg.visitorEmail));
337	    case 'SEND':
338	      return withTokens(async (t) => {
339	        const result = await engine.send(t.idToken, msg.iCalUid, msg.start, msg.end);
340	        // Passes are now issued (or remain issued after an update) → drop this
341	        // event from the nudge surfaces. Prefer the event id we already hold
342	        // locally (the active eid — exactly the key the marked set uses); fall
343	        // back to the engine's echoed providerEventId. Not relying on the echo
344	        // closes a silent gap where a draft without providerEventId would never
345	        // get suppressed.
346	        if (result.activeCount > 0) {
347	          const stored = await chrome.storage.session.get(ACTIVE_EID_KEY);
348	          const eid = stored[ACTIVE_EID_KEY] as string | undefined;
349	          const eventId = (eid && decodeEid(eid)?.eventId) || result.draft.providerEventId;
350	          if (eventId) {
351	            await markHandled(eventId);
352	            await refreshNudgeSurfaces();
353	          }
354	        }
355	        return result;
356	      });
357	    case 'CANCEL_GUEST':
358	      return withIdToken((t) =>
359	        engine.cancelGuest(t, msg.iCalUid, msg.invitationId),
360	      );
361	
362	    case 'LIST_VISITOR_EVENTS':
363	      return withTokens(async (t) => {
364	        // A refresh hiccup (a transient Calendar API error) must not blank the
365	        // list — serve the last-known marked set instead of failing the query.
366	        try {
367	          await runSync(t.accessToken); // refresh before listing
368	        } catch (err) {
369	          console.warn('[auxilio] LIST_VISITOR_EVENTS refresh failed; serving cached set', err);
370	        }
371	        return listMarked();
372	      });
373	
374	    case 'GET_NUDGE_TARGETS':
375	      // Cheap read of the last-synced marked set (no token / no sync) — lets the
376	      // content script seed its banner immediately on page load.
377	      return ok(await listMarked());
378	
379	    case 'SYNC_NOW':
380	      // On-demand sync (page load / tab focus / just left the editor) so the
381	      // banner appears fast instead of waiting up to a minute for the alarm.
382	      await doSync();
383	      return ok({ synced: true });
384	
385	    case 'GET_PANEL_STATE':
386	      return ok({ open: panelConnected });
387	
388	    case 'NAVIGATE_TO_EVENT':
389	      return withTokens(async (t) => {
390	        // Open the event's full EDIT screen (/r/eventedit/<eid>) in the existing
391	        // calendar tab — same tab, no new tab. The button passes a ready eid
392	        // (from the page's data-eventid); the nudge passes eventId to encode
393	        // (sync events only carry the id; primary calendar id = the user's email).
394	        let editEid = msg.eid;
395	        if (!editEid && msg.eventId) {
396	          const email = t.email ?? (await authStatus()).email ?? 'primary';
397	          editEid = encodeEid(msg.eventId, email);
398	        }
399	        // The web editor needs the *real* calendar id in the eid — for the
400	        // primary calendar that's the user's email, not the literal "primary"
401	        // (which the Calendar API accepts but the /eventedit URL can't resolve).
402	        // Sync/nudge eids are encoded with "primary", so rewrite them here.
403	        if (editEid) {
404	          const dec = decodeEid(editEid);
405	          if (dec && (dec.calendarId === 'primary' || !dec.calendarId)) {
406	            const email = t.email ?? (await authStatus()).email;
407	            if (email) editEid = encodeEid(dec.eventId, email);
408	          }
409	        }
410	        // Google's eventedit eid is base64 with padding stripped — normalize the
411	        // raw data-eventid (which may carry `=` padding) to match.
412	        if (editEid) editEid = editEid.replace(/=+$/, '');
413	        if (!editEid) return { navigated: false };
414	        const tabs = await chrome.tabs.query({ url: 'https://calendar.google.com/*' });
415	        const tab = tabs.find((x) => x.active) ?? tabs[0];
416	        if (tab?.id == null || !tab.url) return { navigated: false };
417	        // Build the edit-screen URL from the /u/<n>/ account segment.
418	        const u = tab.url.match(/\/calendar\/(u\/\d+\/)?/);
419	        const userSeg = u?.[1] ?? '';
420	        const url = `https://calendar.google.com/calendar/${userSeg}r/eventedit/${editEid}`;
421	        console.log('[auxilio] navigate →', { from: tab.url, to: url, editEid });
422	        await chrome.tabs.update(tab.id, { url, active: true });
423	        return { navigated: true };
424	      });
425	
426	    default:
427	      return { ok: false, error: 'Unknown request' };
428	  }
429	}
```

```
49	export function VisitPanel() {
50	  // `storedEid` is what the content script is pointing us at (auto-follow). We
51	  // freeze the *displayed* `eid` while busy so following never switches the view
52	  // mid-action (Guard 1); edits already persist on blur (Guard 2).
53	  const storedEid = useActiveEid();
54	  const snapshot = useActiveSnapshot();
55	  const [eid, setEid] = useState<string | null>(storedEid);
56	  const auth = useAuthStatus();
57	  // Picker fuels the "open from list" empty state (and graceful fallback).
58	  const visitorEvents = useVisitorEvents(!eid && !!auth.data?.signedIn);
59	  const resolve = useResolveEvent(eid);
60	  const event = resolve.data;
61	  const draft = useDraft(event);
62	  // Fill real names + photos for email-only guests in the background (People API).
63	  useResolveGuestNames(draft.data);
64	
65	  const patch = usePatchDraft(event?.iCalUid);
66	  const send = useSend(event?.iCalUid, event?.start, event?.end);
67	  const preview = usePreview(event?.iCalUid);
68	
69	  const [previewData, setPreviewData] = useState<{
70	    preview: PreviewResponse;
71	    recipient: string;
72	  } | null>(null);
73	  // Non-structural host edits (name/phone change, template switch) leave no roster
74	  // delta, so we track them against a BASELINE captured lazily — the first time the
75	  // host touches a given guest (or the template), we snapshot its pre-edit value.
76	  // Deriving "edited" as a diff against that baseline means a revert back to the
77	  // original clears it (button deactivates), and a background name resolution that
78	  // fills an UNtouched guest is never mistaken for an edit (it's not in the baseline).
79	  // Refs (not state) so capturing doesn't itself trigger a render; the patch that
80	  // follows re-renders and recomputes the diff. Cleared when the event changes or a
81	  // send succeeds (the draft then reflects reality).
82	  const editBaseline = useRef<Record<string, { name: string; phone: string }>>({});
83	  const templateBaseline = useRef<string | undefined>(undefined);
84	  const resetEditBaseline = () => {
85	    editBaseline.current = {};
86	    templateBaseline.current = undefined;
87	  };
88	  useEffect(resetEditBaseline, [event?.iCalUid]);
89	
90	  // Guard 1: hold the current event while sending / previewing; catch up once idle.
91	  const busy = send.isPending || preview.isPending || !!previewData;
92	  useEffect(() => {
93	    if (!busy) setEid(storedEid);
94	  }, [storedEid, busy]);
95	
96	  // Post-save refresh: the background sync poll broadcasts when the active event
97	  // changed on the server (no DOM); refetch so the roster reflects saved state.
98	  const qc = useQueryClient();
99	  useEffect(() => {
100	    const listener = (msg: { type?: string }) => {
101	      if (msg?.type === REFRESH_ACTIVE) {
102	        qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['resolve'] });
103	        qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['draft'] });
104	      }
105	    };
106	    chrome.runtime.onMessage.addListener(listener);
107	    return () => chrome.runtime.onMessage.removeListener(listener);
108	  }, [qc]);
109	
110	  // Back → clear the active event/snapshot, returning to the home list.
111	  const clearActive = () =>
112	    chrome.storage.session.remove([ACTIVE_EID_KEY, ACTIVE_SNAPSHOT_KEY]);
113	  // Show Back in every screen except the pure home (no event, no snapshot).
114	  const back = eid || snapshot?.magicPresent ? clearActive : undefined;
115	
116	  // ---- gating states ----
117	  if (!eid) {
118	    // New, not-yet-saved visitor event (no eid, but the DOM saw the magic
119	    // address) → preview the detected guests + prompt to save.
120	    if (snapshot?.magicPresent) {
121	      return (
122	        <Shell onBack={back}>
123	          <NoticeState
124	            title="Save the event to issue passes"
125	            body="This event isn’t saved yet, so passes can’t be issued. Add your guests and save it in Google Calendar, then reopen Manage Visitors."
126	            guests={snapshot.guestEmails}
127	          />
128	        </Shell>
129	      );
130	    }
131	    return (
132	      <Shell>
133	        <EmptyState
134	          auth={auth.data}
135	          events={visitorEvents.data}
136	          loading={visitorEvents.isLoading}
137	          onPick={(ev) => {
138	            // Just point the panel at the picked event — no tab navigation. Use
139	            // "Open in Calendar" in the summary to jump to it explicitly.
140	            chrome.storage.session.set({ [ACTIVE_EID_KEY]: ev.eid });
141	          }}
142	        />
143	      </Shell>
144	    );
145	  }
146	  if (isAuthError(resolve.error) || isAuthError(draft.error)) {
147	    return <Shell onBack={back}><SignInGate /></Shell>;
148	  }
149	  if (resolve.isLoading) return <Shell onBack={back}><LoadingState label="Reading event…" /></Shell>;
150	  if (resolve.isError) {
151	    const msg = (resolve.error as Error).message;
152	    if (msg === 'NOT_SAVED') {
153	      return (
154	        <Shell onBack={back}>
155	          <NoticeState
156	            title="Save the event to issue passes"
157	            body="This event isn’t saved yet, so passes can’t be issued. Save it in Google Calendar, then reopen Manage Visitors."
158	            onRetry={() => resolve.refetch()}
159	            guests={snapshot?.guestEmails}
160	          />
161	        </Shell>
162	      );
163	    }
164	    return (
165	      <Shell onBack={back}>
166	        <ErrorState message={msg} onRetry={() => resolve.refetch()} />
167	      </Shell>
168	    );
169	  }
170	  // Event resolved but no iCalUID → the draft query can't run (would sit on a
171	  // skeleton forever). Surface it instead of hanging.
172	  if (event && !event.iCalUid) {
173	    return (
174	      <Shell onBack={back}>
175	        <NoticeState
176	          title="Can’t register from this event"
177	          body="This event has no calendar UID yet. This usually means it is not fully saved. Save it in Google Calendar and try again."
178	          onRetry={() => resolve.refetch()}
179	        />
180	      </Shell>
181	    );
182	  }
183	  if (draft.isError) {
184	    return (
185	      <Shell onBack={back}>
186	        <ErrorState message={(draft.error as Error).message} onRetry={() => draft.refetch()} />
187	      </Shell>
188	    );
189	  }
190	  if (!draft.data) {
191	    return <Shell onBack={back}><RosterSkeleton title={event?.title} /></Shell>;
192	  }
193	
194	  const data = draft.data;
195	  const included = data.roster.filter((g) => g.include);
196	  const sentCount = data.roster.filter((g) => g.status === 'sent').length;
197	
198	  const templates = data.emailTemplates;
199	  // One template for the whole event (engine stores per-guest; we set all guests
200	  // to the chosen key).
201	  const currentTemplate =
202	    data.roster.find((g) => g.emailTemplateKey)?.emailTemplateKey ??
203	    templates.find((t) => t.isDefault)?.key ??
204	    templates[0]?.key;
205	
206	  // What's actually pending. A new/uninvited guest still to get a pass, or a sent
207	  // guest toggled off (a pending revoke), is a STRUCTURAL change visible in the
208	  // roster (and reverts cleanly because it's recomputed here every render).
209	  const pendingNew = data.roster.filter(
210	    (g) => g.include && g.status !== 'sent' && g.status !== 'cancelled',
211	  );
212	  const pendingCancel = data.roster.filter((g) => g.status === 'sent' && !g.include);
213	
214	  // Non-structural edits: a guest whose name/phone now differs from its captured
215	  // baseline, or a template switched away from its baseline. Reverting to the
216	  // baseline value makes this false again.
217	  const edited =
218	    data.roster.some((g) => {
219	      const b = editBaseline.current[g.email];
220	      return b && ((g.name ?? '') !== b.name || (g.phone ?? '') !== b.phone);
221	    }) ||
222	    (templateBaseline.current !== undefined && currentTemplate !== templateBaseline.current);
223	
224	  const hasChanges = pendingNew.length > 0 || pendingCancel.length > 0 || edited;
225	  // Already-issued event with literally nothing to do → no point re-sending.
226	  const nothingToDo = data.materialized && !hasChanges;
227	
228	  const patchGuest = (email: string, edit: Record<string, unknown>) => {
229	    // An include toggle is STRUCTURAL (pendingNew/pendingCancel) — no baseline needed.
230	    // For a name/phone edit, snapshot this guest's PRE-edit values the first time it's
231	    // touched, so a later revert (or a background name fill on an untouched guest)
232	    // diffs correctly.
233	    if ('name' in edit || 'phone' in edit) {
234	      if (!(email in editBaseline.current)) {
235	        const g = data.roster.find((x) => x.email === email);
236	        if (g) editBaseline.current[email] = { name: g.name ?? '', phone: g.phone ?? '' };
237	      }
238	    }
239	    patch.mutate({ guests: [{ email, ...edit }] });
240	  };
241	
242	  const setEventTemplate = (key: string) => {
243	    // Snapshot the effective template before the first switch, so picking the
244	    // original template back clears the diff.
245	    if (templateBaseline.current === undefined) templateBaseline.current = currentTemplate;
246	    patch.mutate({ guests: data.roster.map((g) => ({ email: g.email, emailTemplateKey: key })) });
247	  };
248	
249	  // Review & send: render the real email for a representative included guest,
250	  // then Send/Cancel from the preview.
251	  const reviewEmail = included[0]?.email;
252	  const openReview = () => {
253	    if (!reviewEmail) return;
254	    preview.mutate(reviewEmail, {
255	      onSuccess: (d) => setPreviewData({ preview: d, recipient: reviewEmail }),
256	    });
257	  };
258	
259	  // First send needs at least one included guest; an already-issued event needs a
260	  // real pending change — otherwise the button is irrelevant and stays disabled.
261	  const canSend =
262	    !send.isPending && (data.materialized ? hasChanges : included.length > 0);
263	  const reviewLabel = data.materialized ? 'Review & update passes' : 'Review & send passes';
264	
265	  return (
266	    <Shell onBack={back}>
267	      <div className="enter" style={{ padding: 'var(--space-lg)', display: 'grid', gap: 'var(--space-lg)' }}>
268	        {/* Event summary */}
269	        <Card style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 'var(--space-sm)' }}>
270	          <div className="type-title-lg">{event?.title || 'Untitled event'}</div>
271	          {event?.start && (
272	            <div className="type-body text-muted" style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
273	              <Clock size={15} strokeWidth={2} />
274	              {formatWhen(event.start, event.end)}
275	            </div>
276	          )}
277	          {event?.rooms && event.rooms.length > 0 && (
278	            <div className="type-body text-muted" style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
279	              <DoorOpen size={15} strokeWidth={2} />
280	              <span className="row__ellipsis">{event.rooms.join(', ')}</span>
281	            </div>
282	          )}
283	          {event?.description && (
284	            <div
285	              className="type-label-sm text-muted"
286	              style={{
287	                whiteSpace: 'pre-wrap',
288	                maxHeight: 72,
289	                overflow: 'hidden',
290	                borderTop: '1px solid var(--color-outline-variant)',
291	                paddingTop: 'var(--space-sm)',
292	              }}
293	            >
294	              {event.description}
295	            </div>
296	          )}
297	          {eid && (
298	            <Button
299	              variant="text"
300	              icon={<ExternalLink size={16} strokeWidth={2} />}
301	              onClick={() => void rpc({ type: 'NAVIGATE_TO_EVENT', eid })}
302	              // Negative left margin = the text button's own horizontal padding,
303	              // so the label optically aligns to the card edge while the hit area
304	              // and hover state layer stay intact (MD3 text-button placement).
305	              style={{ justifySelf: 'start', marginLeft: 'calc(-1 * var(--space-md))' }}
306	            >
307	              Open in Calendar
308	            </Button>
309	          )}
310	          {templates.length >= 2 && (
311	            <SelectField
312	              label="Email template (all guests)"
313	              value={currentTemplate}
314	              onChange={(e) => setEventTemplate(e.target.value)}
315	            >
316	              {templates.map((t) => (
317	                <option key={t.key} value={t.key}>
318	                  {t.name}
319	                  {t.isDefault ? ' (default)' : ''}
320	                </option>
321	              ))}
322	            </SelectField>
323	          )}
324	        </Card>
325	
326	        {/* Roster */}
327	        <section>
328	          <div
329	            className="type-label-sm text-muted"
330	            style={{ textTransform: 'uppercase', marginBottom: 4 }}
331	          >
332	            Guests · {included.length} getting a pass
333	          </div>
334	          {data.roster.length === 0 ? (
335	            <Card className="type-body text-muted">
336	              No guests on this event yet. Add attendees in Calendar, then reopen.
337	            </Card>
338	          ) : (
339	            <div className="roster">
340	              {data.roster.map((g) => (
341	                <RosterRow
342	                  key={g.email}
343	                  guest={g}
344	                  onChange={(edit) => patchGuest(g.email, edit)}
345	                />
346	              ))}
347	            </div>
348	          )}
349	        </section>
350	
351	        {/* Send result */}
352	        {send.isSuccess && <SendSummary result={send.data} />}
353	        {send.isError && (
354	          <div className="banner banner--error" role="alert">
355	            <AlertCircle size={18} strokeWidth={2} style={{ flex: '0 0 auto' }} />
356	            <span className="type-body">{(send.error as Error).message}</span>
357	          </div>
358	        )}
359	      </div>
360	
361	      {/* Sticky action bar */}
362	      <footer
363	        style={{
364	          padding: 'var(--space-md) var(--space-lg)',
365	          borderTop: '1px solid var(--color-outline-variant)',
366	          background: 'var(--color-surface-lowest)',
367	        }}
368	      >
369	        <AutoHideBanner show={nothingToDo}>
370	          <div
371	            className="banner banner--info"
372	            role="status"
373	            style={{ marginBottom: 'var(--space-sm)' }}
374	          >
375	            <CheckCircle2 size={18} strokeWidth={2} style={{ flex: '0 0 auto' }} />
376	            <span className="type-body">Up to date. No changes to send.</span>
377	          </div>
378	        </AutoHideBanner>
379	        <Button
380	          block
381	          loading={preview.isPending}
382	          disabled={!canSend}
383	          icon={<Eye size={18} strokeWidth={2} />}
384	          onClick={openReview}
385	        >
386	          {preview.isPending
387	            ? 'Loading preview…'
388	            : `${reviewLabel}${included.length ? ` (${included.length})` : ''}`}
389	        </Button>
390	        {preview.isError && (
391	          <div className="type-label-sm" style={{ color: 'var(--color-error)', textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 6 }}>
392	            {(preview.error as Error).message}
393	          </div>
394	        )}
395	        {sentCount > 0 && !send.isPending && !nothingToDo && (
396	          <div className="type-label-sm text-muted" style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 6 }}>
397	            {sentCount} pass{sentCount > 1 ? 'es' : ''} already issued
398	          </div>
399	        )}
400	      </footer>
401	
402	      {previewData && (
403	        <PreviewSheet
404	          preview={previewData.preview}
405	          recipient={previewData.recipient}
406	          totalCount={included.length}
407	          sending={send.isPending}
408	          onSend={() =>
409	            send.mutate(undefined, {
410	              onSuccess: () => {
411	                setPreviewData(null);
412	                resetEditBaseline();
413	              },
414	            })
415	          }
416	          onClose={() => setPreviewData(null)}
417	        />
418	      )}
419	    </Shell>
420	  );

423	// ───────────────────────── sub-components ─────────────────────────
424	
425	/** Shows its children for ~5s each time `show` flips true, then auto-hides — an
426	 *  ephemeral notice rather than a banner that lingers. Re-shows if `show` cycles. */
427	function AutoHideBanner({ show, children }: { show: boolean; children: React.ReactNode }) {
428	  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(false);
429	  useEffect(() => {
430	    if (!show) {
431	      setVisible(false);
432	      return;
433	    }
434	    setVisible(true);
435	    const t = setTimeout(() => setVisible(false), 5000);
436	    return () => clearTimeout(t);
437	  }, [show]);
438	  return visible ? <>{children}</> : null;
439	}
440	
441	function Shell({ children, onBack }: { children: React.ReactNode; onBack?: () => void }) {
442	  return (
443	    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', height: '100%', minWidth: 320 }}>
444	      <header
445	        style={{
446	          display: 'flex',
447	          alignItems: 'center',
448	          gap: 'var(--space-sm)',
449	          padding: 'var(--space-md) var(--space-lg)',
450	          borderBottom: '1px solid var(--color-outline-variant)',
451	          flex: '0 0 auto',
452	        }}
453	      >
454	        {onBack ? (
455	          <IconButton label="Back to events" onClick={onBack}>
456	            <ArrowLeft size={20} strokeWidth={2} />
457	          </IconButton>
458	        ) : (
459	          <Logo size={26} />
460	        )}
461	        <span className="type-title">Auxilio Visitor</span>
462	        <span style={{ flex: 1 }} />
463	        <AccountMenu />
464	      </header>
465	      <div style={{ flex: 1, minHeight: 0, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', overflowY: 'auto' }}>
466	        {children}
467	      </div>
468	    </div>
469	  );
470	}
471	
472	function LoadingState({ label }: { label: string }) {
473	  return (
474	    <div
475	      style={{
476	        flex: 1,
477	        display: 'flex',
478	        flexDirection: 'column',
479	        alignItems: 'center',
480	        justifyContent: 'center',
481	        gap: 'var(--space-md)',
482	        color: 'var(--color-on-surface-variant)',
483	      }}
484	    >
485	      <span className="spinner" style={{ width: 22, height: 22 }} />
486	      <span className="type-body">{label}</span>
487	    </div>
488	  );
489	}
490	
491	function RosterSkeleton({ title }: { title?: string }) {
492	  return (
493	    <div style={{ padding: 'var(--space-lg)', display: 'grid', gap: 'var(--space-lg)' }}>
494	      <Card style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 8 }}>
495	        <span className="type-title-lg">{title || 'Loading event…'}</span>
496	        <Skeleton width="60%" />
497	      </Card>
498	      <Card style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 'var(--space-lg)' }}>
499	        {[0, 1, 2].map((i) => (
500	          <div key={i} style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 'var(--space-md)', alignItems: 'center' }}>
501	            <Skeleton width={44} height={24} radius={999} />
502	            <div style={{ flex: 1, display: 'grid', gap: 6 }}>
503	              <Skeleton width="50%" />
504	              <Skeleton width="70%" height={12} />
505	            </div>
506	          </div>
507	        ))}
508	      </Card>
509	    </div>
510	  );
511	}
512	
513	function NoticeState({
514	  title,
515	  body,
516	  onRetry,
517	  guests,
518	}: {
519	  title: string;
520	  body: string;
521	  onRetry?: () => void;
522	  guests?: string[];
523	}) {
524	  return (
525	    <div style={{ padding: 'var(--space-lg)', display: 'grid', gap: 'var(--space-md)' }}>
526	      <Card style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 'var(--space-sm)', padding: 'var(--space-xl)' }}>
527	        <span className="type-title-lg">{title}</span>
528	        <span className="type-body text-muted">{body}</span>
529	        {onRetry && (
530	          <Button
531	            variant="tonal"
532	            icon={<RefreshCw size={16} strokeWidth={2} />}
533	            onClick={onRetry}
534	            style={{ justifySelf: 'start', marginTop: 'var(--space-xs)' }}
535	          >
536	            Check again
537	          </Button>
538	        )}
539	      </Card>
540	      {guests && guests.length > 0 && (
541	        <Card style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 'var(--space-xs)' }}>
542	          <span className="type-label-sm text-muted" style={{ textTransform: 'uppercase' }}>
543	            Guests detected on this event
544	          </span>
545	          {guests.map((g) => (
546	            <span key={g} className="type-body row__ellipsis">{g}</span>
547	          ))}
548	        </Card>
549	      )}
550	    </div>
551	  );
552	}
553	
554	function ErrorState({ message, onRetry }: { message: string; onRetry: () => void }) {
555	  return (
556	    <div style={{ padding: 'var(--space-lg)' }}>
557	      <div className="banner banner--error" role="alert" style={{ marginBottom: 'var(--space-md)' }}>
558	        <AlertCircle size={18} strokeWidth={2} style={{ flex: '0 0 auto' }} />
559	        <span className="type-body">{message}</span>
560	      </div>
561	      <Button variant="tonal" icon={<RefreshCw size={16} strokeWidth={2} />} onClick={onRetry}>
562	        Try again
563	      </Button>
564	    </div>
565	  );
566	}
567	
568	function SendSummary({
569	  result,
570	}: {
571	  result: import('@/lib/types').SendResponse;
572	}) {
573	  return (
574	    <div
575	      className="banner"
576	      role="status"
577	      aria-live="polite"
578	      style={{ background: 'var(--color-success-container)', color: 'var(--color-on-success-container)' }}
579	    >
580	      <CheckCircle2 size={18} strokeWidth={2} style={{ flex: '0 0 auto' }} />
581	      <div>
582	        <div className="type-label">
583	          {result.created.length > 0
584	            ? `${result.created.length} pass${result.created.length > 1 ? 'es' : ''} sent`
585	            : result.cancelled.length > 0
586	              ? 'Passes updated'
587	              : 'No changes'}
588	          {result.cancelled.length > 0 && ` · ${result.cancelled.length} cancelled`}
589	        </div>
590	        {result.failed.length > 0 && (
591	          <ul className="type-label-sm" style={{ margin: '4px 0 0', paddingLeft: 16 }}>
592	            {result.failed.map((f) => (
593	              <li key={f.visitorEmail}>
594	                {f.visitorEmail}: {f.reason}
595	              </li>
596	            ))}
597	          </ul>
598	        )}
599	      </div>
600	    </div>
601	  );
602	}
603	
604	function formatWhen(start: string, end?: string): string {
605	  const allDay = !start.includes('T');
606	  const s = new Date(start);
607	  const date = s.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short' });
608	  if (allDay) return `${date} · All day`;
609	  const t = (d: Date) => d.toLocaleTimeString(undefined, { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' });
610	  return end ? `${date} · ${t(s)} to ${t(new Date(end))}` : `${date} · ${t(s)}`;
611	}
```

```
138	function formatWhen(start: string): string {
139	  const allDay = !start.includes('T');
140	  const d = new Date(start);
141	  const date = d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short' });
142	  if (allDay) return `${date} · All day`;
143	  return `${date} · ${d.toLocaleTimeString(undefined, { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' })}`;
144	}
145
```

```
92	// ─────────────────────────── sync loop ───────────────────────────
93	
94	async function doSync(): Promise<void> {
95	  let tokens: { idToken: string; accessToken: string };
96	  try {
97	    tokens = await getValidTokens();
98	  } catch {
99	    // Token unavailable. If the user was connected, this is a recoverable lapse
100	    // (storage.session wiped on restart, or silent renew failed) — surface it so
101	    // nudging never dies silently. If they never connected, stay quiet.
102	    if (await wasConnected()) await setLapsed(true);
103	    return;
104	  }
105	  let result;
106	  try {
107	    result = await runSync(tokens.accessToken);
108	  } catch (err) {
109	    console.warn('[auxilio] sync failed', err);
110	    return;
111	  }
112	
113	  // Got here with working tokens → any prior lapse is over.
114	  await setLapsed(false);
115	
116	  // Cross-channel suppression: ask the engine which marked events already have an
117	  // active pass (issued via the add-on or another device), so we don't nag for
118	  // them. Gated/throttled so this isn't a per-minute engine call.
119	  const engineHandled = await maybePollEngineStatus(tokens.idToken);
120	
121	  // Toolbar badge = distinct visitor events still needing passes (engine-filtered,
122	  // series-collapsed) — sourced from listMarked so it matches the banner exactly.
123	  const targets = await listMarked();
124	  await chrome.action.setBadgeBackgroundColor({ color: BADGE_COLOR });
125	  await chrome.action.setBadgeText({ text: targets.length > 0 ? String(targets.length) : '' });
126	
127	  // OS notification for each newly-discovered visitor event (out-of-tab alert) —
128	  // but never for one the engine has already handled elsewhere.
129	  for (const ev of result.newMarked) {
130	    if (!engineHandled.has((ev.iCalUid ?? '').toLowerCase())) notifyVisitorEvent(ev);
131	  }
132	
133	  // In-page nudge banner: push the current marked set to calendar tabs, plus
134	  // any events that just stopped being pending (deleted/cancelled) so the page
135	  // can purge stale optimistic nudges for them.
136	  broadcastNudge(targets, result.cancelledIds);
137	
138	  // If the event the panel is showing changed (post-save), tell it to refetch.
139	  const stored = await chrome.storage.session.get(ACTIVE_EID_KEY);
140	  const activeEid = stored[ACTIVE_EID_KEY] as string | undefined;
141	  if (activeEid) {
142	    const dec = decodeEid(activeEid);
143	    if (dec && result.changedIds.has(dec.eventId)) broadcastRefreshActive();
144	  }
145	}
146	
147	/** Ask the engine which pending events already have an active pass (handled via
148	 *  any surface), and cache the answer. Throttled: re-polls when the pending set
149	 *  changes, else at most every STATUS_POLL_INTERVAL_MS. Degrades gracefully — if
150	 *  the engine/route is unreachable it keeps the last known set (local-only
151	 *  suppression still works). Returns the (lower-cased) engine-handled set. */
152	async function maybePollEngineStatus(idToken: string): Promise<Set<string>> {
153	  const uids = await listPendingICalUids();
154	  if (uids.length === 0) {
155	    await setEngineHandled([]); // nothing pending → clear the cache
156	    return new Set();
157	  }
158	  const sig = [...uids].sort().join('|');
159	  const store = await chrome.storage.local.get(STATUS_POLL_KEY);
160	  const last = store[STATUS_POLL_KEY] as { at: number; sig: string } | undefined;
161	  const fresh = last && Date.now() - last.at < STATUS_POLL_INTERVAL_MS;
162	  if (last && last.sig === sig && fresh) return readEngineHandled(); // reuse cache
163	
164	  // Stamp the throttle now (before the call) so a missing/erroring route backs off
165	  // instead of retrying every tick. A changed signature still forces a re-poll.
166	  await chrome.storage.local.set({ [STATUS_POLL_KEY]: { at: Date.now(), sig } });
167	  try {
168	    const { active } = await engine.status(idToken, uids);
169	    await setEngineHandled(active);
170	    return new Set(active.map((u) => u.toLowerCase()));
171	  } catch (err) {
172	    console.warn('[auxilio] engine status poll failed; keeping last known', err);
173	    return readEngineHandled();
174	  }
175	}
176	
177	function broadcastPanelState(open: boolean): void {
178	  sendToCalendarTabs({ type: PANEL_STATE, open });
179	}
180	
181	function broadcastNudge(targets: VisitorEventSummary[], cancelled: string[] = []): void {
182	  sendToCalendarTabs({ type: NUDGE_TARGETS, targets, cancelled });
183	}
184	
185	/** Enter/leave the recoverable auth-lapsed state: a distinct toolbar badge plus a
186	 *  broadcast that drives the in-page "Reconnect" banner. Broadcast unconditionally
187	 *  so a calendar tab loaded mid-lapse learns about it on the next sync. */
188	async function setLapsed(lapsed: boolean): Promise<void> {
189	  if (lapsed) {
190	    await chrome.action.setBadgeBackgroundColor({ color: LAPSED_BADGE_COLOR });
191	    await chrome.action.setBadgeText({ text: '!' });
192	  }
193	  sendToCalendarTabs({ type: AUTH_LAPSED, lapsed });
194	}
195	
196	/** Recompute the nudge surfaces from local state (no Calendar round-trip) — used
197	 *  after a send marks an event handled, so the badge/banner drop it immediately. */
198	async function refreshNudgeSurfaces(): Promise<void> {
199	  const targets = await listMarked();
200	  await chrome.action.setBadgeBackgroundColor({ color: BADGE_COLOR });
201	  await chrome.action.setBadgeText({ text: targets.length > 0 ? String(targets.length) : '' });
202	  broadcastNudge(targets);
203	}
204	
205	function sendToCalendarTabs(message: unknown): void {
206	  chrome.tabs
207	    .query({ url: 'https://calendar.google.com/*' })
208	    .then((tabs) => {
209	      for (const t of tabs) {
210	        if (t.id != null) chrome.tabs.sendMessage(t.id, message).catch(() => {});
211	      }
212	    })
213	    .catch(() => {});
214	}
215	
216	function notifyVisitorEvent(ev: VisitorEventSummary): void {
217	  chrome.notifications.create(`auxilio:${ev.eventId}`, {
218	    type: 'basic',
219	    iconUrl: chrome.runtime.getURL('icon/128.png'),
220	    title: 'Visitor event',
221	    message: `${ev.title}. Open Auxilio to register passes.`,
222	    priority: 1,
223	  });
224	}
225

227	
228	const ok = <T,>(data: T): RpcResponse<T> => ({ ok: true, data });
229
```
