```
1	/**
2	 * Typed RPC over chrome.runtime messaging. The side panel (and content script)
3	 * never touch tokens or the network directly — they send these messages and the
4	 * background worker does the work. This keeps the thin-adapter boundary: one
5	 * place holds secrets + network.
6	 */
7	import type {
8	  ActiveEvent,
9	  AuthStatus,
10	  DomEventSnapshot,
11	  DraftPatch,
12	  DraftResponse,
13	  PreviewResponse,
14	  ResolvedPerson,
15	  SendResponse,
16	  VisitDraft,
17	  VisitorEventSummary,
18	} from './types';
19	
20	export type RpcRequest =
21	  | { type: 'AUTH_STATUS' }
22	  | { type: 'AUTH_SIGN_IN' }
23	  | { type: 'AUTH_SIGN_OUT' }
24	  | { type: 'OPEN_PANEL' }
25	  | { type: 'OPEN_FOR_EVENT'; eid: string; snapshot?: DomEventSnapshot }
26	  | { type: 'FOLLOW_EVENT'; eid: string; snapshot?: DomEventSnapshot }
27	  | { type: 'RESOLVE_EVENT'; eid: string }
28	  | { type: 'DRAFT_LOAD'; event: ActiveEvent }
29	  | { type: 'RESOLVE_GUESTS'; emails: string[] }
30	  | { type: 'DRAFT_PATCH'; iCalUid: string; patch: DraftPatch }
31	  | { type: 'PREVIEW'; iCalUid: string; visitorEmail: string }
32	  | { type: 'SEND'; iCalUid: string; start?: string; end?: string }
33	  | { type: 'CANCEL_GUEST'; iCalUid: string; invitationId: string }
34	  | { type: 'LIST_VISITOR_EVENTS' }
35	  | { type: 'GET_NUDGE_TARGETS' }
36	  | { type: 'SYNC_NOW' }
37	  | { type: 'GET_PANEL_STATE' }
38	  | { type: 'NAVIGATE_TO_EVENT'; eventId?: string; eid?: string };
39	
40	/** Maps each request type to its success payload. */
41	export interface RpcResultMap {
42	  AUTH_STATUS: AuthStatus;
43	  AUTH_SIGN_IN: AuthStatus;
44	  AUTH_SIGN_OUT: { signedIn: false };
45	  OPEN_PANEL: { opened: boolean };
46	  OPEN_FOR_EVENT: { opened: boolean };
47	  FOLLOW_EVENT: { followed: boolean };
48	  RESOLVE_EVENT: ActiveEvent;
49	  DRAFT_LOAD: DraftResponse;
50	  RESOLVE_GUESTS: Record<string, ResolvedPerson>;
51	  DRAFT_PATCH: VisitDraft;
52	  PREVIEW: PreviewResponse;
53	  SEND: SendResponse;
54	  CANCEL_GUEST: { invitationId: string; cancelled: boolean };
55	  LIST_VISITOR_EVENTS: VisitorEventSummary[];
56	  GET_NUDGE_TARGETS: VisitorEventSummary[];
57	  SYNC_NOW: { synced: boolean };
58	  GET_PANEL_STATE: { open: boolean };
59	  NAVIGATE_TO_EVENT: { navigated: boolean };
60	}
61	
62	export type RpcResponse<T> =
63	  | { ok: true; data: T }
64	  | { ok: false; error: string; status?: number; needsAuth?: boolean };
65	
66	export class RpcError extends Error {
67	  constructor(
68	    message: string,
69	    readonly status?: number,
70	    readonly needsAuth?: boolean,
71	  ) {
72	    super(message);
73	    this.name = 'RpcError';
74	  }
75	}
76	
77	/** Side-panel-side caller: throws RpcError on failure, returns typed data. */
78	export async function rpc<K extends RpcRequest['type']>(
79	  msg: Extract<RpcRequest, { type: K }>,
80	): Promise<RpcResultMap[K]> {
81	  const res = (await chrome.runtime.sendMessage(msg)) as
82	    | RpcResponse<RpcResultMap[K]>
83	    | undefined;
84	  if (!res) throw new RpcError('No response from background worker');
85	  if (!res.ok) throw new RpcError(res.error, res.status, res.needsAuth);
86	  return res.data;
87	}

90	 *  content-script button click, read reactively by the side panel). */

92	
93	/** storage.session key holding the DOM snapshot for the pending event — lets the
94	 *  panel paint instantly + handle the unsaved (no-API) case. */
95	export const ACTIVE_SNAPSHOT_KEY = 'auxilio.activeSnapshot';
96	
97	/** Background→content broadcast of side-panel open state, so the in-page button
98	 *  hides while the panel is open (no redundant control). */
99	export const PANEL_STATE = '__auxilio_panel_state';
100	
101	/** Port name the side panel connects on mount so background can track open/close. */
102	export const SIDEPANEL_PORT = 'auxilio.sidepanel';
103	
104	/** Background→content broadcast of the visitor events needing passes (drives the
105	 *  in-page nudge banner). */
106	export const NUDGE_TARGETS = '__auxilio_nudge_targets';
107	
108	/** Background→content broadcast: a recoverable auth lapse (the user was connected
109	 *  but a silent token renew failed). Drives the in-page "Reconnect Auxilio" banner
110	 *  so nudging never dies silently. `lapsed:false` clears it once renew succeeds. */
111	export const AUTH_LAPSED = '__auxilio_auth_lapsed';
112	
113	/** One-way background→panel broadcast: the active event changed on the server
114	 *  (post-save), so the panel should refetch. Not an RPC (no response). */
115	export const REFRESH_ACTIVE = '__auxilio_refresh_active';
116	
117	export function broadcastRefreshActive(): void {
118	  chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: REFRESH_ACTIVE }).catch(() => {});
119	}
```

```
import { LogIn } from 'lucide-react';
import { Button, Card } from '@/design/components';
import { useSignIn } from './hooks';

/** Shown when the engine/Calendar call needs a signed-in host. */
export function SignInGate({ reason }: { reason?: string }) {
  const signIn = useSignIn();
  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 'var(--space-lg)' }}>
      <Card
        style={{
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          alignItems: 'center',
          textAlign: 'center',
          gap: 'var(--space-md)',
          padding: 'var(--space-xl)',
        }}
      >
        <span className="type-title-lg">Sign in to continue</span>
        <span className="type-body text-muted">
          {reason ??
            'Connect your Google account to load guests and send visitor passes.'}
        </span>
        <Button
          icon={<LogIn size={18} strokeWidth={2} />}
          loading={signIn.isPending}
          onClick={() => signIn.mutate()}
        >
          Sign in with Google
        </Button>
        {signIn.isError && (
          <span className="type-label-sm" style={{ color: 'var(--color-error)' }}>
            {(signIn.error as Error).message}
          </span>
        )}
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}
```

> AGENT

I've traced both issues end to end. Before I write code, let me confirm the diagnosis, because the thing you reproduced is **not actually a sync failure** — and that changes the fix.

## What's actually happening

**Your repro (create → send passes → refresh → "No upcoming visitor events found") is working as currently designed, not a sync bug.**

When you hit Send, the background does this (`background.ts:351`):

```ts
await markHandled(eventId);   // adds the event to a "handled" overlay
```

Then the homescreen list comes from `listMarked()`, which **deliberately filters out handled events** (`calendar-sync.ts:269`):

```ts
if (handled[m.eventId]) continue;                       // locally sent
if (engineHandled.has(m.iCalUid.toLowerCase())) continue; // sent via any surface
```

So the moment passes are sent, the event is intentionally dropped from the picker, the toolbar badge, the notification, and the in-page nudge. That suppression is the `project_extension_nudge_reliability` behavior — "stop nagging once the work is done." The event was synced fine; it's just hidden because it's complete. The empty-state copy ("They appear here once an event includes the visitor address") then makes it look like sync never saw the event — which is what sent you down the "sync is broken" path.

So the picker today is a **"still needs passes" to-do queue**, not a list of your visitor events. After you finish the only one, the screen is correctly empty — just with misleading copy.

**The auth-expiry issue is real and separate.** `authStatus()` (`auth.ts:141`) only checks `cached.expiresAt > Date.now()` and returns `signedIn:false` the instant the 1-hour access token lapses — it never attempts the silent renew that `getValidTokens()` already knows how to do. So ~once an hour the panel bounces you to the sign-in gate even though a `prompt=none` renew would succeed. I'll fix that regardless — it's unambiguously correct.

The one genuine product decision is the homescreen:

```
147	   *  recurring series be treated as one nudge unit — one badge, one notification,
148	   *  one picker row showing the soonest pending occurrence. */
149	  seriesId?: string;
150	}
```

```json
{
```

```json
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/auxilio-extension/lib/calendar-sync.ts",
  "old_string": "/** Is this event id currently a known visitor event? */\nexport async function isEventMarked(eventId: string): Promise<boolean> {",
  "new_string": "/** Every upcoming visitor event for the side-panel homescreen — the MANAGEMENT\n *  surface. Unlike listMarked (which feeds the nag surfaces and hides handled\n *  events), this INCLUDES sent events tagged `status:'sent'` so a host can reopen\n *  one to update or cancel, and so the screen confirms finished work instead of\n *  going barren after the last send. Series-collapsed to one row: a recurring\n *  series is 'pending' if ANY occurrence in the window still needs passes (shown at\n *  its soonest pending occurrence), otherwise 'sent' (shown at its soonest). */\nexport async function listForPanel(): Promise<PanelVisitorEvent[]> {\n  const [marked, handled, engineHandled] = await Promise.all([\n    readMarked(),\n    readHandled(),\n    readEngineHandled(),\n  ]);\n  const isSent = (m: VisitorEventSummary) =>\n    !!handled[m.eventId] || engineHandled.has((m.iCalUid ?? '').toLowerCase());\n  const earlier = (a: VisitorEventSummary, b?: VisitorEventSummary) =>\n    !b || (a.start ?? '~').localeCompare(b.start ?? '~') < 0;\n\n  const bySeries = new Map<\n    string,\n    { soonestAny: VisitorEventSummary; soonestPending?: VisitorEventSummary }\n  >();\n  for (const m of Object.values(marked)) {\n    const key = m.seriesId ?? m.eventId;\n    const entry = bySeries.get(key) ?? { soonestAny: m };\n    if (earlier(m, entry.soonestAny)) entry.soonestAny = m;\n    if (!isSent(m) && earlier(m, entry.soonestPending)) entry.soonestPending = m;\n    bySeries.set(key, entry);\n  }\n\n  const rows: PanelVisitorEvent[] = [...bySeries.values()].map((e) =>\n    e.soonestPending\n      ? { ...e.soonestPending, status: 'pending' }\n      : { ...e.soonestAny, status: 'sent' },\n  );\n  return rows.sort((a, b) => (a.start ?? '').localeCompare(b.start ?? ''));\n}\n\n/** Is this event id currently a known visitor event? */\nexport async function isEventMarked(eventId: string): Promise<boolean> {"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/auxilio-extension/features/visit/EmptyState.tsx",
  "content": "import { CalendarClock, CheckCircle2, ChevronRight, UserPlus } from 'lucide-react';\nimport { Card, Spinner } from '@/design/components';\nimport type { AuthStatus, PanelVisitorEvent } from '@/lib/types';\nimport { SignInGate } from './SignInGate';\n\n/**\n * Shown when no event is active — the homescreen. A MANAGEMENT surface: it lists\n * both visitor events still needing passes and ones whose passes are already sent\n * (so finished work is confirmed, not silently hidden, and a sent event can be\n * reopened to update or cancel). Doubles as the \"open from list\" fallback if\n * click-detection ever breaks. Signed-out users get the sign-in here too.\n */\nexport function EmptyState({\n  auth,\n  events,\n  loading,\n  onPick,\n}: {\n  auth: AuthStatus | undefined;\n  events: PanelVisitorEvent[] | undefined;\n  loading: boolean;\n  onPick: (ev: PanelVisitorEvent) => void;\n}) {\n  if (!auth) {\n    return (\n      <Centered>\n        <Spinner size={22} />\n      </Centered>\n    );\n  }\n  if (!auth.signedIn) {\n    return <SignInGate reason=\"Sign in to see your visitor events and register passes.\" />;\n  }\n\n  const pending = (events ?? []).filter((e) => e.status === 'pending');\n  const sent = (events ?? []).filter((e) => e.status === 'sent');\n  const firstLoad = loading && !events;\n\n  return (\n    <div style={{ padding: 'var(--space-lg)', display: 'grid', gap: 'var(--space-lg)' }}>\n      <Card\n        style={{\n          display: 'flex',\n          alignItems: 'center',\n          gap: 'var(--space-md)',\n          padding: 'var(--space-lg)',\n        }}\n      >\n        <span\n          style={{\n            display: 'inline-flex',\n            width: 40,\n            height: 40,\n            flex: '0 0 auto',\n            alignItems: 'center',\n            justifyContent: 'center',\n            borderRadius: 'var(--radius-pill)',\n            background: 'var(--color-primary-container)',\n            color: 'var(--color-on-primary-container)',\n          }}\n        >\n          <UserPlus size={20} strokeWidth={2} />\n        </span>\n        <span className=\"type-body text-muted\">\n          Open a Calendar event and click <strong>Register a visitor</strong>, or pick\n          a visitor event below.\n        </span>\n      </Card>\n\n      <Section title=\"Visitor events needing passes\">\n        {firstLoad ? (\n          <Centered>\n            <Spinner size={20} />\n          </Centered>\n        ) : pending.length > 0 ? (\n          <EventList events={pending} onPick={onPick} />\n        ) : (\n          <Card className=\"type-body text-muted\">\n            No events need passes right now. Open a Calendar event and click\n            Register a visitor to send passes.\n          </Card>\n        )}\n      </Section>\n\n      {sent.length > 0 && (\n        <Section title=\"Passes sent\">\n          <EventList events={sent} onPick={onPick} sent />\n        </Section>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction Section({ title, children }: { title: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {\n  return (\n    <section>\n      <div\n        className=\"type-label-sm text-muted\"\n        style={{ textTransform: 'uppercase', marginBottom: 4 }}\n      >\n        {title}\n      </div>\n      {children}\n    </section>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction EventList({\n  events,\n  onPick,\n  sent,\n}: {\n  events: PanelVisitorEvent[];\n  onPick: (ev: PanelVisitorEvent) => void;\n  sent?: boolean;\n}) {\n  return (\n    <Card style={{ paddingTop: 0, paddingBottom: 0 }}>\n      {events.map((ev) => (\n        <button\n          key={ev.eid}\n          className=\"row\"\n          onClick={() => onPick(ev)}\n          style={{\n            width: '100%',\n            background: 'transparent',\n            border: 'none',\n            font: 'inherit',\n            textAlign: 'left',\n            cursor: 'pointer',\n          }}\n        >\n          <CalendarClock\n            size={18}\n            strokeWidth={2}\n            style={{ flex: '0 0 auto', color: 'var(--color-on-surface-variant)' }}\n          />\n          <div className=\"row__grow\">\n            <div className=\"type-label row__ellipsis\">{ev.title}</div>\n            {ev.start && (\n              <div className=\"type-label-sm text-muted\">{formatWhen(ev.start)}</div>\n            )}\n          </div>\n          {sent ? (\n            <span\n              className=\"type-label-sm\"\n              style={{\n                display: 'inline-flex',\n                alignItems: 'center',\n                gap: 4,\n                flex: '0 0 auto',\n                color: 'var(--color-success, var(--color-primary))',\n              }}\n            >\n              <CheckCircle2 size={15} strokeWidth={2} />\n              Sent\n            </span>\n          ) : (\n            <ChevronRight\n              size={18}\n              strokeWidth={2}\n              style={{ flex: '0 0 auto', color: 'var(--color-outline)' }}\n            />\n          )}\n        </button>\n      ))}\n    </Card>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction Centered({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {\n  return (\n    <div\n      style={{\n        display: 'flex',\n        alignItems: 'center',\n        justifyContent: 'center',\n        padding: 'var(--space-xxl)',\n        color: 'var(--color-on-surface-variant)',\n      }}\n    >\n      {children}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction formatWhen(start: string): string {\n  const allDay = !start.includes('T');\n  const d = new Date(start);\n  const date = d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short' });\n  if (allDay) return `${date} · All day`;\n  return `${date} · ${d.toLocaleTimeString(undefined, { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' })}`;\n}\n"
}
```

Now the auth-expiry fix. Let me update `authStatus()` to attempt the silent renew before declaring the user signed out:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01E3U5MWb8MQxJtEun6jEGgJ
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/auxilio-extension/lib/auth.ts",
  "old_string": "export async function authStatus(): Promise<AuthStatus> {\n  const cached = await readCache();\n  if (cached && cached.expiresAt > Date.now()) {\n    return { signedIn: true, email: cached.email };\n  }\n  return { signedIn: false };\n}",
  "new_string": "export async function authStatus(): Promise<AuthStatus> {\n  const cached = await readCache();\n  if (cached && cached.expiresAt > Date.now()) {\n    return { signedIn: true, email: cached.email };\n  }\n  // Cached access token expired (it lives in storage.session, ~1h, and is wiped on\n  // browser restart). Before reporting signed-out — which bounces the panel to the\n  // sign-in gate roughly every hour — try the same silent (prompt=none) renew the\n  // network path uses, but only if the user actually connected before. A successful\n  // renew keeps them signed in transparently as long as the Google session lives;\n  // only a genuine failure (revoked / really signed out) falls through to the gate.\n  if (await wasConnected()) {\n    try {\n      const bundle = await getValidTokens();\n      return { signedIn: true, email: bundle.email };\n    } catch {\n      return { signedIn: false };\n    }\n  }\n  return { signedIn: false };\n}"
}
```

> AGENT

Done. Here's what I found and changed.

## The key finding: your repro wasn't a sync failure

When you sent passes, the background ran `markHandled(eventId)`, and the homescreen list (`listMarked()`) **deliberately hides handled events** — that's the "stop nagging once done" behavior. So the event synced fine; it was intentionally hidden, and the old empty-state copy ("they appear here once an event includes the visitor address") made that look like sync never saw it. That false signal is what sent you chasing the sync bug again.

## What I changed (extension v0.8.4 → v0.9.0)

**1. Homescreen is now a management surface (the direction you picked)**
- New `listForPanel()` (`lib/calendar-sync.ts`) returns **both** pending and sent visitor events, each tagged `status`. Series-collapsed: a recurring event is `pending` if *any* occurrence still needs passes, else `sent`.
- `LIST_VISITOR_EVENTS` now uses it; `EmptyState.tsx` renders two sections — **"Visitor events needing passes"** and **"Passes sent"** (with a green ✓ Sent tag). You can reopen a sent event to update or cancel.
- The **nag surfaces stay suppressed** — toolbar badge, OS notification, and in-page banner still use `listMarked()`, so finished events don't badge or nag. That split is the principle: nag surfaces hide done work; the panel shows it.
- Empty-pending copy reworded so it no longer implies a sync failure.

**2. Auth-expiry fix (the real, separate bug)**
- `authStatus()` (`lib/auth.ts`) used to return `signedIn: false` the instant the ~1h access token lapsed, bouncing you to the sign-in gate every hour. It now attempts the silent `prompt=none` renew (via `getValidTokens()`) first when you `wasConnected()`, and only falls through to the gate on a genuine failure (revoked / actually signed out).

`npm run compile` and `npm run build` both pass clean.

**To verify:** reload the unpacked extension at `chrome://extensions` (confirm it shows **0.9.0**), reopen the side panel. Your sent event should now appear under **"Passes sent."** One caveat to expect: a brand-new event only lands in the list after a sync captures it — opening the panel triggers a sync first, so it should be there on open, but if a sync was mid-flight give it the ~1-minute alarm tick.

One thing I did **not** touch: the deeper stale-token / attendee-trim sync analysis in `Planning-docs/2026-06-23_…visitor_event_sync_visibility_bug.md` is a genuinely separate concern. If you ever see an event that you *haven't* sent passes for stay missing, that's the one to reopen — watch the console for `[auxilio] sync MISS …`, which would point at Google trimming attendees in `events.list`.

```
1	/**
2	 * Content script on Google Calendar — the DOM layer of the hybrid model.
3	 *
4	 * It reads the open event surface (detail popover / expanded edit) to:
5	 *  - GATE the "Manage Visitors" button on the magic address being a guest,
6	 *  - capture the `data-eventid` (base64url "<eventId> <calendarId>"), and
7	 *  - take a best-effort SNAPSHOT (guest emails) so the panel can paint instantly
8	 *    and cover the unsaved (no-API) case.
9	 * The API layer (background) remains the authoritative source. We read only the
10	 * semantic `data-eventid`, the `[role="dialog"]` ARIA signal, and the surface's
11	 * visible text — no layout/CSS-class coupling, no injection into Google's markup.
12	 */
13	import { MAGIC_ADDRESS } from '@/lib/config';
14	import {
15	  ACTIVE_EID_KEY,
16	  ACTIVE_SNAPSHOT_KEY,
17	  AUTH_LAPSED,
18	  NUDGE_TARGETS,
19	  PANEL_STATE,
20	} from '@/lib/messaging';
21	import type { DomEventSnapshot, VisitorEventSummary } from '@/lib/types';
22	
23	/** True while our extension context is still valid. After an extension reload or
24	 *  update, this content script is orphaned: every `chrome.*` call then throws
25	 *  "Extension context invalidated" (synchronously — `.catch()` can't see it). We
26	 *  check this before touching any chrome API so an orphan stops quietly. */
27	const extAlive = (): boolean => {
28	  try {
29	    return !!chrome.runtime?.id;
30	  } catch {
31	    return false;
32	  }
33	};
34	
35	/** sendMessage that never throws or rejects, even once the context is gone. */
36	function safeSend<T = unknown>(message: unknown): Promise<T | undefined> {
37	  if (!extAlive()) return Promise.resolve(undefined);
38	  try {
39	    return Promise.resolve(chrome.runtime.sendMessage(message)).catch(() => undefined);
40	  } catch {
41	    return Promise.resolve(undefined);
42	  }
43	}
44	
45	/** storage.session helpers that no-op once the context is gone. */
46	function safeStorageGet(key: string): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
47	  if (!extAlive()) return Promise.resolve({});
48	  try {
49	    return Promise.resolve(chrome.storage.session.get(key)).catch(() => ({}));
50	  } catch {
51	    return Promise.resolve({});
52	  }
53	}
54	function safeStorageSet(items: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<void> {
55	  if (!extAlive()) return Promise.resolve();
56	  try {
57	    return Promise.resolve(chrome.storage.session.set(items)).catch(() => {});
58	  } catch {
59	    return Promise.resolve();
60	  }
61	}
62	
63	export default defineContentScript({
64	  matches: ['https://calendar.google.com/*'],
65	  main() {
66	    // The id of the most recently clicked event chip — the event whose popover
67	    // is opening. Reset when the surface closes so a later unrelated dialog
68	    // can't resurrect a stale button.
69	    let clickedEid: string | null = null;
70	    let lastSnapshot: DomEventSnapshot | null = null;
71	    let panelOpen = false;
72	    // The dialog node we've accepted as an event surface. Held so recognition is
73	    // sticky for the life of that node (see readSurface) — kills the button flicker.
74	    let eventDialog: HTMLElement | null = null;
75	    let followedEid: string | null = null; // last event auto-pushed to the panel
76	    let pendingMagic: { eid: string; eventId: string; title: string } | null = null;
77	    let everSawEventId = false;
78	
79	    // Button UI: a native injected button when a clean anchor exists, else a
80	    // floating fallback (Idea 2). The magic-address gate is dropped — the button
81	    // shows on any open event surface; clicking it IS the intent.
82	    const button = createButtonUI(() => {
83	      if (!lastSnapshot) return;
84	      // Always hand the click to the background as OPEN_FOR_EVENT. The
85	      // background is the single authority on whether the panel is connected
86	      // (it owns the panel's port): it calls sidePanel.open only when the panel
87	      // is closed, and otherwise just points the open panel at this event. We
88	      // deliberately do NOT branch on a local panelOpen/opening flag here — those
89	      // drift out of sync (a missed PANEL_STATE, or a failed open leaving a latch
90	      // stuck) and were the reason a click sometimes silently did nothing.
91	      openFor(lastSnapshot);
92	      // Note: we intentionally do NOT navigate the tab to the event here. The
93	      // panel resolves the event itself (Calendar API), so reloading the page
94	      // adds nothing but cost + surprise. "Open in Calendar" in the panel is the
95	      // explicit, user-initiated way to jump to the event.
96	    });
97	
98	    // In-page nudge banner: visitor events the user saved but hasn't acted on.
99	    // Driven by the background sync (robust), not live DOM scanning.
100	    const nudge = mountNudge();
101	    safeSend<{ ok?: boolean; data?: VisitorEventSummary[] }>({ type: 'GET_NUDGE_TARGETS' }).then(
102	      (res) => {
103	        if (res?.ok) nudge.setTargets(res.data as VisitorEventSummary[]);
104	      },
105	    );
106	
107	    // Reconnect banner: shown when the background reports a recoverable auth lapse
108	    // (the user was connected but a silent token renew failed), so nudging never
109	    // dies silently. The background broadcasts AUTH_LAPSED on each sync.
110	    const reconnect = mountReconnect(() => safeSend({ type: 'OPEN_PANEL' }));
111	
112	    // Seed the panel-open state on (re)load so auto-follow works after a
113	    // same-tab navigation (the panel persists; this content script is fresh).
114	    safeSend<{ ok?: boolean; data?: { open: boolean } }>({ type: 'GET_PANEL_STATE' }).then((res) => {
115	      if (res?.ok) {
116	        panelOpen = !!res.data?.open;
117	        nudge.setPanelOpen(panelOpen);
118	        reconnect.setPanelOpen(panelOpen);
119	        render();
120	      }
121	    });
122	
123	    // Fast detection: sync on load, on tab focus, and right after leaving the
124	    // event editor (a likely save) — so the banner appears in seconds, not up
125	    // to a minute, and shows immediately when the user returns to the tab.
126	    let lastSync = 0;
127	    const syncNow = () => {
128	      const now = Date.now();
129	      if (now - lastSync < 4000) return; // throttle
130	      lastSync = now;
131	      safeSend({ type: 'SYNC_NOW' });
132	    };
133	    // Burst: fire several syncs after a likely save to beat Google's API
134	    // eventual-consistency lag (a fresh events.list may not show the just-saved
135	    // event for a few seconds).
136	    let lastBurst = 0;
137	    const syncBurst = () => {
138	      const now = Date.now();
139	      if (now - lastBurst < 2000) return;
140	      lastBurst = now;
141	      for (const d of [0, 3000, 8000]) {
142	        setTimeout(() => safeSend({ type: 'SYNC_NOW' }), d);
143	      }
144	    };
145	    syncNow();
146	    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
147	      if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') syncNow();
148	    });
149	    window.addEventListener('focus', syncNow);
150	    let wasEditor = /\/eventedit/.test(location.pathname);
151	    const editorPoll = setInterval(() => {
152	      if (!extAlive()) return teardown(); // orphaned by an extension reload → stop
153	      const isEditor = /\/eventedit/.test(location.pathname);
154	      if (wasEditor && !isEditor) syncBurst(); // left the editor → likely saved
155	      wasEditor = isEditor;
156	      // Safety net: re-assert the button. The MutationObserver is debounced, so a
157	      // continuous scroll (which fires mutations faster than the debounce) can
158	      // starve it and never re-run render after Google re-renders the modal and
159	      // drops our injected button. This guarantees it comes back within ~1s.
160	      render();
161	    }, 1000);
162	
163	    // Stop all work once our context is invalidated (extension reloaded/updated),
164	    // so an orphaned script doesn't keep observing/polling — and remove our now-
165	    // dead button (its click can't reach the background) instead of leaving a
166	    // zombie that looks live but does nothing.
167	    let disposed = false;
168	    function teardown() {
169	      if (disposed) return;
170	      disposed = true;
171	      clearInterval(editorPoll);
172	      observer.disconnect();
173	      button.update(false);
174	    }
175	
176	    /** Read the open event surface, or null if none is open. `eid` may be '' for
177	     *  a brand-new, not-yet-saved event (no data-eventid / URL eid yet). */
178	    function readSurface(): { el: HTMLElement; eid: string } | null {
179	      const fromUrl = urlEid();
180	      const dialog = document.querySelector('[role="dialog"]') as HTMLElement | null;
181	      // A dialog that isn't an event surface — e.g. Google's "Send update emails
182	      // to existing guests?" save confirmation — must NEVER host our button, and
183	      // while one is open we suppress entirely so we don't inject onto it (or
184	      // behind it, over the editor). This is what kept the button appearing on
185	      // unrelated dialogs and "flashing" away when such a dialog opened/closed.
186	      if (dialog) {
187	        // Sticky recognition: once a dialog node is accepted as an event
188	        // surface, keep treating it as one until it actually leaves the DOM.
189	        // Google re-renders the popover's innards constantly; isEventDialog is a
190	        // heuristic, so a single tick where it momentarily reads false would
191	        // otherwise yank the button and pop it back a frame later — the flicker.
192	        // A genuinely different dialog (a confirmation) is a different node, so
193	        // it's still evaluated strictly and correctly rejected.
194	        const isEvent = dialog === eventDialog || isEventDialog(dialog);
195	        if (!isEvent) return null;
196	        eventDialog = dialog;
197	      } else {
198	        eventDialog = null;
199	      }
200	      if (fromUrl) {
201	        return { el: dialog ?? document.body, eid: fromUrl };
202	      }
203	      // Full-screen create/edit editor. An existing event always carries its eid
204	      // in the URL (handled above), so reaching here means a brand-new, unsaved
205	      // event → eid ''. Never fall back to clickedEid: it can be stale from a
206	      // previously viewed event and would point the panel at the wrong one.
207	      if (/\/eventedit/.test(location.pathname)) {
208	        return { el: dialog ?? document.body, eid: '' };
209	      }
210	      // Detail / quick-create popover.
211	      if (dialog) return { el: dialog, eid: clickedEid ?? '' };
212	      return null;
213	    }
214	
215	    function snapshotOf(el: HTMLElement, eid: string): DomEventSnapshot {
216	      const text = el.innerText || '';
217	      const lower = text.toLowerCase();
218	      const magicPresent = !!MAGIC_ADDRESS && lower.includes(MAGIC_ADDRESS);
219	      const guestEmails = extractEmails(text).filter(
220	        (e) => e.toLowerCase() !== MAGIC_ADDRESS,
221	      );
222	      // Best-effort title (cosmetic — the API sync replaces it). The full editor
223	      // exposes the title as a text input; the detail popover (the common case)
224	      // shows it as a heading, not an input — so fall back to the open dialog's
225	      // heading / accessible name. Scoped to the dialog so we never grab the
226	      // page's own H1 ("Calendar") when no event surface is open.
227	      const titleInput = el.querySelector(
228	        'input[aria-label*="title" i], input[placeholder*="title" i]',
229	      ) as HTMLInputElement | null;
230	      const dialog = el.matches?.('[role="dialog"]')
231	        ? el
232	        : el.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[role="dialog"]');
233	      const heading = dialog?.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[role="heading"], h1, h2');
234	      const title =
235	        titleInput?.value?.trim() ||
236	        heading?.innerText?.trim() ||
237	        dialog?.getAttribute('aria-label')?.trim() ||
238	        undefined;
239	      return { eid, magicPresent, guestEmails, title };
240	    }
241	
242	    function render() {
243	      if (!extAlive()) return teardown(); // orphaned by an extension reload → stop
244	      const surface = readSurface();
245	      lastSnapshot = surface ? snapshotOf(surface.el, surface.eid) : null;
246	
247	      // Instant nudge: while viewing a visitor event WITH a real eid, remember
248	      // it; when the user then leaves that surface (closed popover / saved &
249	      // left editor), show the nudge for it IMMEDIATELY — no API round-trip.
250	      if (lastSnapshot?.magicPresent && lastSnapshot.eid) {
251	        pendingMagic = {
252	          eid: lastSnapshot.eid,
253	          eventId: decodeEventId(lastSnapshot.eid),
254	          title: lastSnapshot.title ?? '',
255	        };
256	      } else if (!surface && pendingMagic) {
257	        nudge.addOptimistic({
258	          eid: pendingMagic.eid,
259	          eventId: pendingMagic.eventId,
260	          iCalUid: '',
261	          title: pendingMagic.title,
262	          start: undefined,
263	        });
264	        syncBurst(); // confirm/refresh from the API right after
265	        pendingMagic = null;
266	      }
267	
268	      // Show the button on any open event surface — including while the panel is
269	      // open. We keep the floating fallback available even then: a missing button
270	      // reads as broken (the user's report), and the native in-popover button can
271	      // lose the first-open injection race. The grace period in update() still
272	      // lets the native button win whenever it injects in time, so the FAB only
273	      // appears as a genuine last resort. (We no longer navigate the tab on click,
274	      // so the old "flash while a navigated page loads" concern is moot.)
275	      button.update(!!surface);
276	      if (panelOpen) maybeFollow();
277	    }
278	
279	    /** Push the open *saved* event to the panel so it follows what you're viewing.
280	     *  Saved only (non-empty eid); deduped; the panel guards against switching
281	     *  mid-action. */
282	    function maybeFollow() {
283	      const snap = lastSnapshot;
284	      if (!snap || !snap.eid) return; // new/unsaved events don't auto-switch
285	      if (snap.eid === followedEid) return;
286	      followedEid = snap.eid;
287	      safeSend({ type: 'FOLLOW_EVENT', eid: snap.eid, snapshot: snap });
288	    }
289	
290	    document.addEventListener(
291	      'click',
292	      (e) => {
293	        const chip = (e.target as Element | null)?.closest?.('[data-eventid]');
294	        const id = chip?.getAttribute('data-eventid');
295	        if (id) {
296	          everSawEventId = true;
297	          clickedEid = id;
298	        }
299	        // Re-check a few times: Google paints the popover progressively, so a
300	        // single 60ms probe can land before the surface (or its rows) exist,
301	        // and we'd then wait up to a second for the safety-net poll. Staggered
302	        // probes catch a slow paint quickly; render() is idempotent + cheap.
303	        for (const d of [60, 250, 600]) setTimeout(render, d);
304	      },
305	      true,
306	    );
307	
308	    document.addEventListener(
309	      'keydown',
310	      (e) => {
311	        if (e.key === 'Escape') {
312	          clickedEid = null;
313	          setTimeout(render, 60);
314	        }
315	      },
316	      true,
317	    );
318	
319	    // Detect surface open/close + edits (e.g. adding the magic address live).
320	    let timer: number | undefined;
321	    const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
322	      clearTimeout(timer);
323	      timer = setTimeout(() => {
324	        if (!document.querySelector('[role="dialog"]') && !urlEid()) {

326	        }
327	        render();
328	      }, 150) as unknown as number;
329	    });
330	    observer.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });

332	    window.addEventListener('popstate', render);

336	      (msg: {
337	        type?: string;
338	        open?: boolean;
339	        targets?: VisitorEventSummary[];
340	        cancelled?: string[];
341	        lapsed?: boolean;
342	      }) => {
343	        if (msg?.type === PANEL_STATE) {

345	          if (!panelOpen) followedEid = null; // re-follow next time it opens
346	          nudge.setPanelOpen(panelOpen); // banner hides while the panel is open
347	          reconnect.setPanelOpen(panelOpen);
348	          render();
349	        } else if (msg?.type === NUDGE_TARGETS) {
350	          nudge.setTargets(msg.targets ?? [], msg.cancelled ?? []);
351	        } else if (msg?.type === AUTH_LAPSED) {
352	          reconnect.setLapsed(!!msg.lapsed);
353	        }
354	      },
355	    );

357	    setTimeout(() => {
358	      if (!everSawEventId && !document.querySelector('[data-eventid]')) {
359	        console.warn(
360	          '[auxilio] no [data-eventid] found — click detection may be unavailable; use the panel’s event list.',
361	        );
362	      }
363	    }, 90_000);
364	
365	    render();
366	    console.log('[auxilio] content script ready on Google Calendar');

368	});

370	function openFor(snapshot: DomEventSnapshot) {

372	}
373	
374	/** Event id from the expanded-edit URL path, if present. */
375	function urlEid(): string | null {
376	  const path = location.pathname.match(/\/eventedit\/([^/?#]+)/);
377	  if (path && path[1] !== 'eventedit') return safeDecode(path[1]);
378	  const q = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('eid');
379	  return q ? safeDecode(q) : null;
380	}
381	
382	/** Pull unique email-looking strings from text (best-effort guest scrape). */
383	function extractEmails(text: string): string[] {
384	  const matches = text.match(/[a-z0-9._%+-]+@[a-z0-9.-]+\.[a-z]{2,}/gi) ?? [];
385	  return [...new Set(matches.map((m) => m.toLowerCase()))];
386	}
387	
388	/** eid (base64url of "<eventId> <calendarId>") → eventId, for nudge dedup. */
389	function decodeEventId(eid: string): string {
390	  try {
391	    let b64 = eid.replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/');
392	    b64 += '='.repeat((4 - (b64.length % 4)) % 4);
393	    return atob(b64).split(' ')[0] || '';
394	  } catch {
395	    return '';
396	  }
397	}
398	
399	function safeDecode(s: string): string {
400	  try {
401	    return decodeURIComponent(s);
402	  } catch {
403	    return s;
404	  }
405	}

407	const USERS_SVG = `
408	<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24"
409	  fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">
410	  <path d="M16 21v-2a4 4 0 0 0-4-4H6a4 4 0 0 0-4 4v2"/><circle cx="9" cy="7" r="4"/>
411	  <path d="M22 21v-2a4 4 0 0 0-3-3.87"/><path d="M16 3.13a4 4 0 0 1 0 7.75"/></svg>`;
412	
413	const LABEL = 'Manage Visitors';
414	const INJECT_ID = 'auxilio-manage-visitors';
415	
416	/**
417	 * Button UI manager: injects a native-styled "Manage Visitors" button as a real,
418	 * full-width row in the surface — below the Availability/Visibility section on the
419	 * edit page, at the bottom of the outermost content list in the detail popover (so
420	 * it scrolls with the rest of the UI, never an overlay) — and falls back to a
421	 * floating button only when no anchor is found. `update(show)` decides per render.
422	 */
423	function createButtonUI(onClick: () => void) {
424	  const fab = mountFloating(onClick);
425	  let injected: HTMLElement | null = null;
426	  let retryTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
427	
428	  // A clean injection anchor: where to drop the button + how far to indent it so
429	  // it lines up with the section's content column (past Google's icon gutter).
430	  // `inline` = sit next to a native button instead of as its own row.
431	  type Anchor = { insert: (el: HTMLElement) => void; inset: number; inline?: boolean };
432	
433	  /** Text/ARIA-based only — never CSS classes — so we stay decoupled from
434	   *  Google's obfuscated markup. Places the button where it reads as part of the
435	   *  form: between Description and the next section on the edit page, and as the
436	   *  last row of the detail popover's content list. Floating only as last resort. */
437	  function findAnchor(): Anchor | null {
438	    // Full-screen edit page → our own row just BELOW the Availability/Visibility
439	    // info section (falling back to above Description).
440	    if (/\/eventedit/.test(location.pathname)) {
441	      // Primary: an inline button right before the native "Save" in the editor's
442	      // action bar, so it sits next to Save / More options and reads as a native
443	      // inline action — what the host wants on a new/unsaved event.
444	      const save = findButtonByText('save');
445	      const saveParent = save?.parentElement;
446	      if (save && saveParent) {
447	        return { insert: (el) => saveParent.insertBefore(el, save), inset: 0, inline: true };
448	      }
449	      // Fallback: our own full-width row just BELOW the Availability/Visibility info
450	      // section (else above Description) when the action bar can't be found.
451	      const avail = findAvailabilityInfo();
452	      const afterAvail = avail ? rowsListFor(avail) : null;
453	      if (afterAvail) {
454	        const { list, row } = afterAvail;
455	        return { insert: (el) => list.insertBefore(el, row.nextSibling), inset: rowInset(row, list) };
456	      }
457	      const desc = findEditDescription();
458	      const aboveDesc = desc ? rowsListFor(desc) : null;
459	      if (aboveDesc) {
460	        const { list, row } = aboveDesc;
461	        return { insert: (el) => list.insertBefore(el, row), inset: rowInset(row, list) };
462	      }
463	      return null;
464	    }
465	    // Detail popover / modal → BOTTOM of the OUTERMOST content list (after every
466	    // section, including the guest list). Robust whether or not guests exist.
467	    const dialog = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[role="dialog"]');
468	    if (dialog) {
469	      // Quick-create popover carries a "Save" (and usually "More options"). Sit
470	      // inline right before it so we read as one of its action buttons — the same
471	      // inline feel as the editor. A saved-event DETAIL popover has no "Save"
472	      // (Edit/Delete instead), so this never fires there and we keep the row at the
473	      // bottom of its content list.
474	      const save = findButtonByText('save');
475	      if (save && dialog.contains(save) && save.parentElement) {
476	        const parent = save.parentElement;
477	        return { insert: (el) => parent.insertBefore(el, save), inset: 0, inline: true };
478	      }
479	      const list = findContentList(dialog);
480	      if (list) {
481	        const ref = firstIconLedRow(list);
482	        return { insert: (el) => list.appendChild(el), inset: ref ? rowInset(ref, list) : 0 };
483	      }
484	      // Fallback → bottom of the scrolling content.
485	      const scope = scrollContainer(dialog) ?? dialog;
486	      const l = mainVerticalList(scope) ?? scope;
487	      return { insert: (el) => l.appendChild(el), inset: 0 };
488	    }
489	    return null;
490	  }
491	
492	  function ensureInjected(): boolean {
493	    if (injected && injected.isConnected) return true; // still placed — skip the scan
494	    const anchor = findAnchor();
495	    if (!anchor) return false;
496	    removeInjected();
497	    ensureInjectedStyles();
498	    injected = anchor.inline
499	      ? buildInlineButton(onClick)
500	      : buildInjectedRow(onClick, anchor.inset);
501	    anchor.insert(injected);
502	    return true;
503	  }
504	
505	  function removeInjected() {
506	    injected?.remove();
507	    injected = null;
508	  }
509	
510	  return {
511	    // `allowFab` gates the floating fallback. It's only there to surface the
512	    // panel when we can't inject a native button; with the panel already open
513	    // it's redundant (auto-follow handles the viewed event), and it would flash
514	    // at the corner while a navigated page is still loading. So callers pass
515	    // false when the panel is open.
516	    update(show: boolean, allowFab = true) {
517	      clearTimeout(retryTimer);
518	      if (!show) {
519	        removeInjected();
520	        fab.setVisible(false);
521	        return;
522	      }
523	      // Try to inject now; if the anchor isn't in the DOM / laid out yet (Google
524	      // is still rendering the surface), keep retrying for a few seconds. The
525	      // MutationObserver covers DOM changes, but layout can settle — giving
526	      // elements their width — without a childList mutation, so width-based
527	      // anchor checks can miss that window. This retry guarantees we don't.
528	      let attempts = 0;
529	      const attempt = () => {
530	        const placed = ensureInjected();
531	        // Show the floating fallback only after a short grace period, so on a
532	        // normally-rendering surface the native button just appears — no flash
533	        // of the FAB getting replaced a moment later.
534	        fab.setVisible(allowFab && !placed && attempts >= 3);
535	        if (placed || attempts++ >= 30) return;
536	        retryTimer = setTimeout(attempt, 150);
537	      };
538	      attempt();
539	    },
540	  };
541	}
542	
543	/**
544	 * Is this open `[role="dialog"]` an actual event surface (detail popover / quick
545	 * edit), rather than one of Google's transient confirmation dialogs — e.g.
546	 * "Send update emails to existing Google Calendar guests?" or "Delete this
547	 * event?" — which we must not decorate? Positive, stable signals only: an event
548	 * chip id, the magic address in its text, or several icon-led section rows

550	 * row of action buttons, so it matches none of these.
551	 */
552	function isEventDialog(dialog: HTMLElement): boolean {
553	  if (dialog.querySelector('[data-eventid]')) return true;

556	  return !!findContentList(dialog);

558	
559	/** Find a clickable element whose visible text matches (case-insensitive). */
560	function findButtonByText(text: string): HTMLElement | null {
561	  const wanted = text.toLowerCase();
562	  const els = document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('button,[role="button"]');
563	  for (const el of els) {
564	    if ((el.textContent ?? '').trim().toLowerCase() === wanted) return el;
565	  }
566	  return null;
567	}
568	
569	const isVisible = (el: HTMLElement) => el.offsetParent !== null || el.getClientRects().length > 0;
570	
571	/** The Description editor on the event edit page. ARIA/placeholder text only —
572	 *  never CSS classes (Google's are obfuscated and churn). */
573	function findEditDescription(): HTMLElement | null {
574	  const els = document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
575	    '[aria-label*="description" i], [placeholder*="description" i]',
576	  );
577	  for (const el of els) if (isVisible(el)) return el;
578	  return null;
579	}
580	
581	/** The Availability/Visibility info block on the edit page. Prefers Google's
582	 *  stable `jsname` hook, falling back to the info text. */
583	function findAvailabilityInfo(): HTMLElement | null {
584	  const byJsname = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[jsname="g7cnnb"]');
585	  if (byJsname && isVisible(byJsname)) return byJsname;
586	  for (const el of document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('span,div')) {
587	    const t = (el.textContent ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();
588	    if (t.startsWith('availability might be shown') && isVisible(el)) return el;
589	  }
590	  return null;
591	}
592	
593	/** The OUTERMOST rows list inside `root`: the shallowest container whose own
594	 *  children include several icon-led section rows. Breadth-first so a nested
595	 *  guest list never wins over the main content list. */
596	function findContentList(root: HTMLElement): HTMLElement | null {
597	  const queue: HTMLElement[] = [];
598	  for (const c of root.children) if (c instanceof HTMLElement) queue.push(c);
599	  while (queue.length) {
600	    const el = queue.shift()!;
601	    if (!isVisible(el)) continue;
602	    if (iconLedRowCount(el) >= 2) return el;
603	    for (const c of el.children) if (c instanceof HTMLElement) queue.push(c);
604	  }
605	  return null;
606	}
607	
608	/** The first wide icon-led row in a list — a reference for measuring the text
609	 *  column inset. */
610	function firstIconLedRow(list: HTMLElement): HTMLElement | null {
611	  const lw = list.getBoundingClientRect().width;
612	  for (const c of list.children) {
613	    if (!(c instanceof HTMLElement) || !isVisible(c) || !looksLikeRow(c)) continue;
614	    if (lw > 0 && c.getBoundingClientRect().width < lw * 0.5) continue;
615	    return c;
616	  }
617	  return null;
618	}
619	
620	/** Does this element stack its visible children vertically (a column of rows),

622	function stacksVertically(el: HTMLElement): boolean {
623	  const kids = [...el.children].filter((c): c is HTMLElement => c instanceof HTMLElement && isVisible(c));
624	  if (kids.length < 2) return false;
625	  for (let i = 1; i < kids.length; i++) {
626	    const a = kids[i - 1].getBoundingClientRect();
627	    const b = kids[i].getBoundingClientRect();
628	    if (b.top >= a.bottom - 2) return true; // the next child starts below the previous
629	  }
630	  return false;
631	}
632	
633	/** A native Calendar content row = a block whose first child is a decorative
634	 *  (aria-hidden) leading icon, followed by content. This is the one stable,
635	 *  class-free signal Google keeps across both the popover and the edit page. */
636	function looksLikeRow(el: HTMLElement): boolean {
637	  const first = el.firstElementChild;
638	  return (
639	    !!first &&
640	    el.childElementCount >= 2 &&
641	    (first.getAttribute('aria-hidden') === 'true' ||
642	      !!first.querySelector('svg, img, i.google-material-icons'))

644	}
645	
646	/** How many of a container's visible children are icon-led *section* rows.
647	 *  Rows must span most of the container's width, which excludes narrow icon
648	 *  clusters like a description formatting toolbar. */
649	function iconLedRowCount(container: HTMLElement): number {
650	  const cw = container.getBoundingClientRect().width;
651	  let n = 0;
652	  for (const c of container.children) {
653	    if (!(c instanceof HTMLElement) || !isVisible(c) || !looksLikeRow(c)) continue;
654	    if (cw > 0 && c.getBoundingClientRect().width < cw * 0.5) continue; // skip toolbars/chips
655	    n++;
656	  }
657	  return n;
658	}
659	
660	/** From a field/label, climb to the rows list that holds it: the first ancestor
661	 *  whose parent contains several icon-led rows. Returns that list plus the row
662	 *  (a direct child of the list) that contains `el` — so we can insert relative
663	 *  to it. Null if no rows list is recognised. */
664	function rowsListFor(el: HTMLElement): { list: HTMLElement; row: HTMLElement } | null {
665	  let node: HTMLElement = el;
666	  while (node.parentElement && node.parentElement !== document.body) {
667	    const parent = node.parentElement;
668	    if (iconLedRowCount(parent) >= 2) return { list: parent, row: node };

670	  }
671	  return null;
672	}
673	
674	/** The biggest vertically-stacking container inside `root` — the content column
675	 *  whose last child is the visual bottom of the surface. */
676	function mainVerticalList(root: HTMLElement): HTMLElement | null {
677	  let best: HTMLElement | null = null;
678	  let bestArea = 0;
679	  for (const el of root.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('*')) {
680	    if (!isVisible(el) || !stacksVertically(el)) continue;
681	    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
682	    const area = r.width * r.height;
683	    if (area > bestArea) {
684	      bestArea = area;
685	      best = el;
686	    }
687	  }
688	  return best;
689	}
690	
691	/** Left indent (px) that lines our button up with a native row's *text* column,
692	 *  past Google's leading icon gutter. Measured from where the row's first text
693	 *  actually paints, relative to the list we insert into. */
694	function rowInset(row: HTMLElement, list: HTMLElement): number {
695	  const gap = firstTextLeft(row) - list.getBoundingClientRect().left;
696	  return Number.isFinite(gap) && gap > 0 && gap < 240 ? Math.round(gap) : 0;
697	}
698	
699	/** Left edge of the first painted text inside `el` (skips the decorative icon
700	 *  gutter), or the element's own left if it has none. */
701	function firstTextLeft(el: HTMLElement): number {
702	  const walker = document.createTreeWalker(el, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);
703	  let node: Node | null;
704	  while ((node = walker.nextNode())) {
705	    if (!node.textContent || !node.textContent.trim()) continue;
706	    const range = document.createRange();
707	    range.selectNodeContents(node);
708	    const rect = range.getBoundingClientRect();
709	    if (rect.width > 0) return rect.left;
710	  }
711	  return el.getBoundingClientRect().left;
712	}
713	
714	/** The scrolling content region inside a surface (overflow-y auto/scroll),
715	 *  largest first — so our row lives with the content and scrolls, not in a
716	 *  pinned header/footer. Null when nothing scrolls. */
717	function scrollContainer(root: HTMLElement): HTMLElement | null {
718	  let best: HTMLElement | null = null;
719	  let bestArea = 0;
720	  for (const el of root.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('*')) {
721	    if (!isVisible(el)) continue;
722	    const oy = getComputedStyle(el).overflowY;
723	    if (oy !== 'auto' && oy !== 'scroll') continue;
724	    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
725	    const area = r.width * r.height;
726	    if (area > bestArea) {
727	      bestArea = area;
728	      best = el;
729	    }
730	  }
731	  return best;
732	}

734	/** Stop our interactions dismissing Google's modal / triggering its buttons. */
735	function shieldInteractions(el: HTMLElement) {
736	  for (const type of ['pointerdown', 'mousedown', 'touchstart'] as const) {
737	    el.addEventListener(
738	      type,
739	      (e) => {
740	        e.stopPropagation();
741	        e.preventDefault(); // don't move focus out of the Calendar dialog
742	      },
743	      true,
744	    );
745	  }
746	  el.addEventListener('click', (e) => e.stopPropagation(), true);
747	}
748	
749	const STYLE_ID = 'auxilio-manage-visitors-style';
750	
751	/** One-time stylesheet for the injected (light-DOM) button. Styled to match a
752	 *  native Material outlined Calendar button — same shape, size, typography and
753	 *  hover state-layer — so it reads as part of Google's UI, just brand-tinted. */
754	function ensureInjectedStyles() {
755	  if (document.getElementById(STYLE_ID)) return;
756	  const s = document.createElement('style');
757	  s.id = STYLE_ID;
758	  s.textContent = `
759	    .auxilio-mv-row{box-sizing:border-box;display:flex;align-items:center;width:100%;padding:8px 0;}
760	    .auxilio-mv-btn{
761	      box-sizing:border-box;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;
762	      font-family:'Google Sans','Roboto',Arial,sans-serif;font-size:14px;font-weight:500;
763	      line-height:20px;letter-spacing:.25px;color:#92288e;background:transparent;
764	      border:1px solid #747775;border-radius:999px;padding:8px 18px 8px 14px;
765	      min-height:40px;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap;
766	      transition:background-color 120ms ease,border-color 120ms ease;
767	    }
768	    .auxilio-mv-btn:hover{background:rgba(146,40,142,.08);}
769	    .auxilio-mv-btn:active{background:rgba(146,40,142,.12);}
770	    .auxilio-mv-btn:focus-visible{outline:2px solid #92288e;outline-offset:2px;}
771	    .auxilio-mv-btn[disabled]{opacity:.6;cursor:default;}
772	    .auxilio-mv-btn svg{width:18px;height:18px;flex:0 0 auto;}
773	  `;
774	  document.documentElement.appendChild(s);
775	}
776	
777	/** The native-styled button itself (light DOM, classes from ensureInjectedStyles). */
778	function makeNativeButton(onClick: () => void): HTMLButtonElement {
779	  const btn = document.createElement('button');
780	  btn.type = 'button';
781	  btn.className = 'auxilio-mv-btn';
782	  btn.innerHTML = `${USERS_SVG}<span>${LABEL}</span>`;
783	  shieldInteractions(btn);
784	  wireActivate(btn, onClick);
785	  return btn;
786	}
787	
788	/** A full-width row carrying the button, indented to align with the section
789	 *  content column. Flows like a native section, so it can't overlap siblings. */
790	function buildInjectedRow(onClick: () => void, inset: number): HTMLElement {
791	  const row = document.createElement('div');
792	  row.id = INJECT_ID;
793	  row.className = 'auxilio-mv-row';
794	  if (inset) row.style.paddingLeft = `${inset}px`;
795	  row.appendChild(makeNativeButton(onClick));
796	  // Guard only the row's empty padding — clicks there must not dismiss Google's
797	  // surface; the button manages its own interactions (shieldInteractions).
798	  shieldPadding(row);
799	  return row;
800	}
801	
802	/** Inline button that sits in the editor / quick-create action bar, next to the
803	 *  native "More options" / "Save". As an inline-flex box it would otherwise align
804	 *  to the row's text baseline (riding slightly high with a gap beneath); center it
805	 *  for both the inline-block case (vertical-align) and the flex-row case (align-self
806	 *  — so it doesn't stretch taller than its neighbours either). */
807	function buildInlineButton(onClick: () => void): HTMLButtonElement {
808	  const btn = makeNativeButton(onClick);
809	  btn.id = INJECT_ID;
810	  btn.style.marginRight = '8px';
811	  btn.style.verticalAlign = 'middle';
812	  btn.style.alignSelf = 'center';
813	  return btn;
814	}
815	
816	/** Stop interactions on the row's own padding (but not the button) from reaching
817	 *  Google — so an accidental tap beside the button can't close the dialog. */
818	function shieldPadding(row: HTMLElement) {
819	  const guard = (e: Event) => {
820	    if (e.target === row) {
821	      e.preventDefault();
822	      e.stopPropagation();
823	    }
824	  };
825	  for (const type of ['pointerdown', 'mousedown', 'touchstart', 'click'] as const) {
826	    row.addEventListener(type, guard, true);
827	  }
828	}
829	
830	/** Floating fallback button (shadow-DOM, fully isolated). */
831	function mountFloating(onClick: () => void) {
832	  const host = document.createElement('div');
833	  // pointer-events:none so the host box never eats clicks on what's beneath it when
834	  // the FAB is hidden — the inner button is display:inline-flex even when invisible, so
835	  // the host keeps a bounding box at bottom-right that would otherwise sit over (and
836	  // swallow clicks for) Google's own bottom drawer buttons (e.g. the event-edit add-on
837	  // drawer's "View"). The shown button re-enables hits via `.fab.show{pointer-events:auto}`.

869	/** Shared activate handler (pointerup + click, double-fire-guarded). */
870	function wireActivate(btn: HTMLButtonElement, onClick: () => void) {
871	  const label = btn.querySelector('span:last-of-type') as HTMLSpanElement | null;
872	  let firing = false;
873	  const activate = async () => {
874	    if (firing) return;
875	    firing = true;
876	    btn.disabled = true;
877	    const prev = label?.textContent;
878	    if (label) label.textContent = 'Opening…';
879	    try {
880	      await onClick();
881	    } finally {
882	      btn.disabled = false;
883	      if (label && prev) label.textContent = prev;
884	      firing = false;
885	    }
886	  };
887	  btn.addEventListener('pointerup', activate);
888	  btn.addEventListener('click', activate);
889	}

906	function mountNudge() {
907	  const host = document.createElement('div');
908	  // pointer-events:none so the (always-present) host box doesn't swallow clicks on the

914	  document.documentElement.appendChild(host);
915	  const root = host.attachShadow({ mode: 'open' });
916	  root.innerHTML = `
917	    <style>
918	      :host { all: initial; }
919	      .banner {
920	        display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;max-width:520px;
921	        font-family:'Google Sans','Roboto',system-ui,sans-serif;color:#310031;

923	        padding:10px 12px 10px 16px;box-shadow:0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,.18);
924	        transform:translateY(-12px);opacity:0;pointer-events:none;
925	        transition:opacity 200ms cubic-bezier(.2,0,0,1),transform 200ms cubic-bezier(.2,0,0,1);
926	      }
927	      .banner.show{opacity:1;transform:none;pointer-events:auto;}
928	      .icon{display:inline-flex;color:#92288e;flex:0 0 auto;}
929	      .text{font-size:13.5px;line-height:18px;}
930	      .text b{font-weight:600;}
931	      .actions{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;flex:0 0 auto;}
932	      .manage{font:inherit;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;color:#fff;background:#92288e;
933	        border:none;border-radius:999px;padding:8px 14px;cursor:pointer;}
934	      .manage:hover{box-shadow:0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,.2);}
935	      .dismiss{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;width:30px;height:30px;
936	        border:none;border-radius:50%;background:transparent;color:#5f5560;cursor:pointer;}
937	      .dismiss:hover{background:#0000000f;}
938	      @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce){.banner{transition:none;transform:none;}}

940	    <div class="banner">
941	      <span class="icon">${BELL_SVG}</span>
942	      <span class="text"></span>
943	      <span class="actions">
944	        <button class="manage" type="button">Manage</button>
945	        <button class="dismiss" type="button" aria-label="Dismiss">${NUDGE_X_SVG}</button>
946	      </span>

949	  const text = root.querySelector('.text') as HTMLSpanElement;
950	  const manageBtn = root.querySelector('.manage') as HTMLButtonElement;
951	  const dismissBtn = root.querySelector('.dismiss') as HTMLButtonElement;
952	  shieldInteractions(host);
953	
954	  let targets: VisitorEventSummary[] = [];
955	  // Optimistic, DOM-derived targets shown INSTANTLY (no API wait) for an event
956	  // the user just interacted with; the API sync reconciles them by eventId.
957	  const optimistic = new Map<string, VisitorEventSummary>();
958	  // Each optimistic guess is provisional: if the sync never confirms it as a
959	  // real visitor event (e.g. the event was deleted, already handled, or the
960	  // magic address removed), it self-expires rather than lingering forever.
961	  const optimisticTimers = new Map<string, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>();
962	  const OPTIMISTIC_TTL = 12_000;
963	  const dropOptimistic = (eventId: string) => {
964	    const timer = optimisticTimers.get(eventId);
965	    if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
966	    optimisticTimers.delete(eventId);
967	    optimistic.delete(eventId);
968	  };
969	  let panelOpen = false;
970	  let dismissed = new Set<string>();
971	  let current: VisitorEventSummary | null = null;
972	
973	  safeStorageGet(NUDGE_DISMISS_KEY).then((r) => {
974	    dismissed = new Set((r[NUDGE_DISMISS_KEY] as string[]) ?? []);
975	    render();
976	  });
977	
978	  /** Merged, deduped, soonest-first (synced target wins over optimistic). */
979	  function merged(): VisitorEventSummary[] {
980	    const byId = new Map<string, VisitorEventSummary>();
981	    for (const o of optimistic.values()) byId.set(o.eventId, o);
982	    for (const t of targets) byId.set(t.eventId, t); // synced data wins

986	  }
987	
988	  function visible(): VisitorEventSummary | null {
989	    return merged().find((t) => !dismissed.has(t.eventId)) ?? null;
990	  }
991	
992	  function render() {
993	    const t = panelOpen ? null : visible();
994	    current = t;
995	    if (!t) {
996	      wrap.classList.remove('show');
997	      return;
998	    }
999	    const more = merged().filter((x) => !dismissed.has(x.eventId)).length - 1;
1000	    text.innerHTML =
1001	      `<b>Visitor event needs passes.</b> ${escapeText(t.title || '')}` +
1002	      (more > 0 ? ` <span style="opacity:.7">+${more} more</span>` : '');
1003	    wrap.classList.add('show');

1006	  // Use pointerup (not click): the host's capture-phase shield — which stops our
1007	  // interactions from dismissing Google's modal — would otherwise swallow click.
1008	  manageBtn.addEventListener('pointerup', () => {
1009	    if (!current) return;
1010	    const target = current;
1011	    manageBtn.disabled = true;
1012	    manageBtn.textContent = 'Opening…';
1013	    // Engaging with the event clears its nudge for the session, so it doesn't
1014	    // reappear when the panel closes (or when the optimistic re-add fires).
1015	    dismissed.add(target.eventId);
1016	    safeStorageSet({ [NUDGE_DISMISS_KEY]: [...dismissed] });
1017	    render(); // hide the banner now
1018	    // Set the active event FIRST (storage.session, now content-accessible) so the
1019	    // panel has it the moment it mounts — no dependence on message ordering.
1020	    safeStorageSet({ [ACTIVE_EID_KEY]: target.eid, [ACTIVE_SNAPSHOT_KEY]: null });

1022	    // resolves the event on its own; "Open in Calendar" there is the explicit way
1023	    // to jump to it.
1024	    safeSend({ type: 'OPEN_FOR_EVENT', eid: target.eid }).finally(() => {
1025	      manageBtn.disabled = false;
1026	      manageBtn.textContent = 'Manage';
1027	    });
1028	  });
1029	
1030	  dismissBtn.addEventListener('pointerup', () => {
1031	    if (!current) return;
1032	    dismissed.add(current.eventId);
1033	    safeStorageSet({ [NUDGE_DISMISS_KEY]: [...dismissed] });
1034	    render();
1035	  });

1037	  return {
1038	    setTargets(next: VisitorEventSummary[], cancelled: string[] = []) {
1039	      targets = next ?? [];
1040	      // The sync authoritatively resolved these away (deleted/cancelled/no
1041	      // longer a visitor event) → purge any optimistic guess so a deleted
1042	      // event can't keep nudging (or reopen the panel for a ghost event).
1043	      for (const id of cancelled) dropOptimistic(id);
1044	      // Drop optimistic entries the real sync now covers.
1045	      for (const t of targets) dropOptimistic(t.eventId);
1046	      render();
1047	    },
1048	    addOptimistic(s: VisitorEventSummary) {

1050	      const existing = optimisticTimers.get(s.eventId);
1051	      if (existing) clearTimeout(existing);

1053	        s.eventId,
1054	        setTimeout(() => {

1056	          optimisticTimers.delete(s.eventId);
1057	          render();
1058	        }, OPTIMISTIC_TTL),
1059	      );

1061	    },
1062	    setPanelOpen(open: boolean) {
1063	      panelOpen = open;
1064	      render();
1065	    },
1066	  };
1067	}
```

```
1	import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
2	import { ChevronDown, Phone, User } from 'lucide-react';
3	import { Chip, Switch, TextField } from '@/design/components';
4	import type { DraftGuest } from '@/lib/types';
5	
6	type GuestEdit = {
7	  include?: boolean;
8	  name?: string;
9	  phone?: string;
10	};
11	
12	/** Two-letter monogram from a name ("Jane Doe" → "JD") or an email local-part
13	 *  ("jane.doe@x" → "JD"), so each guest has a stable, recognisable avatar. */
14	function monogram(seed: string): string {
15	  const parts = seed.trim().split(/[\s._-]+/).filter(Boolean);
16	  if (parts.length === 0) return '?';
17	  if (parts.length === 1) return parts[0].slice(0, 2).toUpperCase();
18	  return (parts[0][0] + parts[1][0]).toUpperCase();
19	}
20	
21	export function RosterRow({
22	  guest,
23	  onChange,
24	}: {
25	  guest: DraftGuest;
26	  onChange: (edit: GuestEdit) => void;
27	}) {
28	  const [name, setName] = useState(guest.name);
29	  const [phone, setPhone] = useState(guest.phone ?? '');
30	  // Details stay collapsed by default so each card is compact; the host opens
31	  // them only to make a correction. Sent guests are editable too — re-sending
32	  // pushes the change onto the already-issued pass.
33	  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
34	
35	  // Reconcile server/background updates WITHOUT clobbering a live edit: adopt the
36	  // new server value only when the local field still equals the last server value
37	  // we saw (the host hasn't typed over it). So a background name/photo resolve fills
38	  // an untouched field, but a half-typed correction is never lost. (See the contacts
39	  // name-resolution flow — the name can change after first paint.)
40	  // Capture the PREVIOUS server value before mutating the ref: setName's functional
41	  // updater runs lazily (next commit), so reassigning the ref first would make the
42	  // updater compare against the new value and never adopt it. Snapshot prev, point
43	  // the ref at the new value, then compare the updater's local to that snapshot.
44	  const lastServerName = useRef(guest.name);
45	  useEffect(() => {
46	    const prev = lastServerName.current;
47	    lastServerName.current = guest.name;
48	    setName((local) => (local === prev ? guest.name : local));
49	  }, [guest.name]);
50	
51	  const lastServerPhone = useRef(guest.phone ?? '');
52	  useEffect(() => {
53	    const next = guest.phone ?? '';
54	    const prev = lastServerPhone.current;
55	    lastServerPhone.current = next;
56	    setPhone((local) => (local === prev ? next : local));
57	  }, [guest.phone]);
58	
59	  const sent = guest.status === 'sent';
60	  const cancelled = guest.status === 'cancelled';
61	  const display = guest.name || guest.email;
62	  // A sent guest toggled OFF is a pending cancel — re-sending will revoke their
63	  // pass (engine applyDraft cancels !include guests with an active pass), so we
64	  // dim the row and don't offer edits. Everyone we'd still issue/update a pass
65	  // for can be corrected, whether or not their pass already went out.
66	  const pendingCancel = sent && !guest.include;
67	  const editable = guest.include && !cancelled;
68	
69	  // The drawer handle names exactly what's still missing, so the host knows what
70	  // tapping it is for. A real name is absent when the engine flagged the name as the
71	  // email-derived fallback; phone is absent when blank.
72	  const hasName = !guest.nameIsFallback;
73	  const hasPhone = !!guest.phone?.trim();
74	  const drawerLabel = editing
75	    ? 'Hide details'
76	    : hasName && hasPhone
77	      ? 'Edit details'
78	      : !hasName && !hasPhone
79	        ? 'Add phone and name'
80	        : hasPhone
81	          ? 'Add name'
82	          : 'Add phone';
83	
84	  // Switch is no longer locked once a pass is sent: toggling off cancels on the
85	  // next update (mirrors the add-on), toggling back on re-issues.
86	  const switchLabel = sent
87	    ? guest.include
88	      ? `Cancel the pass for ${guest.email}`
89	      : `Re-issue a pass for ${guest.email}`
90	    : guest.include
91	      ? `Don’t invite ${guest.email}`
92	      : `Invite ${guest.email}`;
93	
94	  return (
95	    <div className={`guest${guest.include ? '' : ' guest--dim'}`}>
96	      <div className="guest__head">
97	        <span
98	          className={
99	            'guest__avatar' +
100	            (pendingCancel
101	              ? ' guest__avatar--dim'
102	              : sent
103	                ? ' guest__avatar--sent'
104	                : guest.include
105	                  ? ''
106	                  : ' guest__avatar--dim')
107	          }
108	          aria-hidden
109	        >
110	          {guest.photoUrl ? (
111	            <img className="guest__photo" src={guest.photoUrl} alt="" loading="lazy" />
112	          ) : (
113	            monogram(guest.name || guest.email.split('@')[0])
114	          )}
115	        </span>
116	
117	        <div className="guest__id">
118	          <div className="guest__name">
119	            <span className="type-label row__ellipsis">{display}</span>
120	            {guest.internal && !sent && !cancelled && <Chip>Internal</Chip>}
121	            {sent && guest.include && <Chip tone="success">Pass sent</Chip>}
122	            {pendingCancel && <Chip tone="error">Will cancel</Chip>}
123	            {cancelled && <Chip tone="error">Cancelled</Chip>}
124	          </div>
125	          <div className="type-label-sm text-muted row__ellipsis">{guest.email}</div>
126	        </div>
127	
128	        <Switch
129	          checked={guest.include}
130	          onChange={(v) => onChange({ include: v })}
131	          label={switchLabel}
132	        />
133	      </div>
134	
135	      {editable ? (
136	        // Full-bleed "scroll" drawer pinned to the card's bottom edge: a subtle,
137	        // edge-to-edge handle the host pulls open only when they need to correct
138	        // a detail — keeps each card compact by default.
139	        <div className={`guest__drawer${editing ? ' guest__drawer--open' : ''}`}>
140	          <button

144	            onClick={() => setEditing((v) => !v)}
145	          >

147	            {drawerLabel}

160	                label="Phone"

163	                leadingIcon={<Phone size={16} strokeWidth={2} />}
164	                onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
165	                onBlur={() => phone !== (guest.phone ?? '') && onChange({ phone })}
166	                placeholder="+91…"
167	              />
168	            </div>
169	          )}
170	        </div>
171	      ) : (
172	        !guest.include &&
173	        !cancelled && (
174	          <div className="guest__hint type-label-sm">Will not receive a pass. Toggle on to invite.</div>
175	        )
176	      )}
177	    </div>
178	  );
179	}
180
```

```
1	/**
2	 * Engine client (background only) for the office-ops-engine /addon/* routes.
3	 * Reuses the existing contract verbatim — no engine changes. Authenticated with
4	 * the host's Google id_token (Bearer). Always sends the canonical iCalUid so the
5	 * extension converges on the same calendar_event_links row as .ics/OAuth/add-on.
6	 */
7	import { ADDON_API } from './config';
8	import type {
9	  ActiveEvent,
10	  DraftPatch,
11	  DraftResponse,
12	  PreviewResponse,
13	  SendResponse,
14	  VisitDraft,
15	} from './types';
16	
17	export class EngineError extends Error {
18	  constructor(
19	    message: string,
20	    readonly status: number,
21	  ) {
22	    super(message);
23	    this.name = 'EngineError';
24	  }
25	}
26	
27	async function call<T>(
28	  path: string,
29	  method: string,
30	  idToken: string,
31	  body?: unknown,
32	): Promise<T> {
33	  const url = `${ADDON_API}/${path}`;
34	  const started = Date.now();
35	  // Abort hung requests so the UI errors instead of spinning forever
36	  // (bifrost dev cold-starts + MV3 service-worker lifetime can stall a fetch).
37	  const ctrl = new AbortController();
38	  const timeout = setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), 20_000);
39	  console.log('[auxilio] engine →', method, url);
40	  let res: Response;
41	  try {
42	    res = await fetch(url, {
43	      method,
44	      headers: {
45	        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
46	        Authorization: `Bearer ${idToken}`,
47	      },
48	      body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined,
49	      signal: ctrl.signal,
50	    });
51	  } catch (err) {
52	    if ((err as Error).name === 'AbortError') {
53	      throw new EngineError(
54	        `Engine did not respond within 20s (${url}). Is it reachable?`,
55	        0,
56	      );
57	    }
58	    throw new EngineError(
59	      `Could not reach the engine (${(err as Error).message}). Check WXT_ENGINE_URL / host_permissions.`,
60	      0,
61	    );
62	  } finally {
63	    clearTimeout(timeout);
64	  }
65	  const text = await res.text();
66	  console.log('[auxilio] engine ←', res.status, `${Date.now() - started}ms`, text.slice(0, 200));
67	  let data: { error?: string } = {};
68	  try {
69	    data = text ? JSON.parse(text) : {};
70	  } catch {
71	    if (!res.ok) throw new EngineError(`Engine error ${res.status}: ${text.slice(0, 140)}`, res.status);
72	  }
73	  if (!res.ok) {
74	    throw new EngineError(data?.error ?? `Request failed (${res.status})`, res.status);
75	  }
76	  return data as T;
77	}
78	
79	export const engine = {
80	  loadDraft: (idToken: string, ev: ActiveEvent) =>
81	    call<DraftResponse>('draft', 'POST', idToken, {
82	      iCalUid: ev.iCalUid,
83	      providerEventId: ev.providerEventId,
84	      title: ev.title,
85	      attendees: ev.attendees,
86	      start: ev.start,
87	      end: ev.end,
88	      location: ev.location,
89	      organizerEmail: ev.organizerEmail,
90	    }),
91	
92	  patchDraft: (idToken: string, iCalUid: string, patch: DraftPatch) =>
93	    call<VisitDraft>('draft', 'PATCH', idToken, { iCalUid, patch }),
94	
95	  preview: (idToken: string, iCalUid: string, visitorEmail: string) =>
96	    call<PreviewResponse>('preview', 'POST', idToken, { iCalUid, visitorEmail }),
97	
98	  send: (idToken: string, iCalUid: string, start?: string, end?: string) =>
99	    call<SendResponse>('send', 'POST', idToken, { iCalUid, start, end }),
100	
101	  /** Read-only batch check: which of these events already have an active pass
102	   *  (through any surface). Lets the background poll suppress cross-channel nudges.
103	   *  Side-effect-free on the engine — safe to poll. */
104	  status: (idToken: string, iCalUids: string[]) =>
105	    call<{ active: string[] }>('status', 'POST', idToken, { iCalUids }),
106	
107	  cancelGuest: (idToken: string, iCalUid: string, invitationId: string) =>
108	    call<{ invitationId: string; cancelled: boolean }>(
109	      'cancel-guest',
110	      'POST',
111	      idToken,
112	      { iCalUid, invitationId },
113	    ),
114	
115	  connectCalendar: (idToken: string, code: string, redirectUri: string) =>
116	    call<{ connected: boolean }>('connect-calendar', 'POST', idToken, { code, redirectUri }),
117	};
```
